\displaystyle C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\|_{H^{5}_{0}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{\gamma-1}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta,\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{6}_{0}}^{6})\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{\gamma-1}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa,\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{11});

\displaystyle|J_{5,5}|=

\displaystyle 2\varepsilon|\int\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}(\frac{\partial_{xy}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}})\cdot w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dx|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon C_{\delta}\|w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\partial_{xy}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle\times\|\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-\sigma_{1}-1}\frac{\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon C_{\delta,\gamma,\sigma_{1}}\|w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\partial_{xy}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle\times(\|\partial_{x}w_{g}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta,\gamma,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{4}),

where we have used the relation

\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}=\partial_{x}^{7}w^{\varepsilon}-g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{x}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}=w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\{\frac{\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\}-\partial_{x}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon},

to establish the following estimate

\displaystyle\|\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-\sigma_{1}-1}\frac{\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\gamma,\sigma_{1}}\|\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-\sigma_{1}}\partial_{y}\{\frac{\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta,\gamma,\sigma_{1}}\|\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\{\frac{\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta,\gamma,\sigma_{1}}(\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}_{1}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta,\gamma,\sigma_{1}}(\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}).

Thus, the combination of estimates of terms from

J_{5,1}
to
J_{5,5}
yields directly

|J_{5}|\leq\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\nu_{3},\kappa,\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{19}).

(3.36)

Deal with
J_{6}
term.

First of all, let us decompose the quantity
Q_{4}
as follows:

Q_{4}\overset{def}{=}Q_{4,1}+Q_{4,2}+Q_{4,3}+Q_{4,4},

where
Q_{4,i}(i=1,2,3,4)
is defined as follows:

\displaystyle Q_{4,1}

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{y}\left\{-u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}-v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\right\};

\displaystyle Q_{4,2}

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}-\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\left(-u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}-v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})\right);

\displaystyle Q_{4,3}

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}u^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\partial_{xy}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\right)+v^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{(\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\right);

\displaystyle Q_{4,4}

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\left\{\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\right\}+\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}).

Therefore,

J_{6}=\sum_{1\leq i\leq 4}\int Q_{4,i}\cdot\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\cdot w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy\overset{def}{=}\sum_{1\leq i\leq 4}J_{6,i}.

It is easy to check that for
i=1,2,3
,

\displaystyle|J_{6,i}|

\displaystyle\leq\|Q_{4,i}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\gamma}\|Q_{4,i}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}.

Let us first deal with estimate
\|Q_{4,i}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}(i=1,2,3)
.
Using the inequality (
A.3
), it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\|Q_{4,1}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}(\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{xy}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}u^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}(\|\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|v^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+C_{\delta,\kappa}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{H^{2}}+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{xy}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(1+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{1}})+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{1}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{1}}+C_{\delta,\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{0}}^{4})\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta,\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{3}_{0}}^{3})\|\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta,\kappa}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{3}).

Similarly, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\|Q_{4,2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|u^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|v^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+C_{\delta,\kappa}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta,\kappa}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2}),

and

\displaystyle\|Q_{4,3}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}\|u^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{xy}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|v^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2}).

Thus, the combination of estimates of terms from

Q_{4,1}
to
Q_{4,3}
yields directly

\sum_{1\leq i\leq 3}|J_{6,i}|\leq C_{\kappa,\delta}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{5}).

(3.37)

Finally, let us deal with
J_{6,4}
. Integrating by part and using the estimate of
J_{5,5}
, we have

\displaystyle|-\varepsilon\int\partial_{x}^{2}\left\{\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\right\}\cdot\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\cdot w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\varepsilon\int\partial_{x}\left\{\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\right\}\cdot\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}\cdot w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy+\varepsilon\int\partial_{x}\left\{\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\right\}\cdot\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon}{4}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta,\gamma,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{4})

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}\left\{\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\right\}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon}{4}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta,\gamma,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{4})+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{xy}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\varepsilon\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta,\gamma,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{4}),

and

\displaystyle|\int\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{y}^{2}\left\{\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\rho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\right\}\cdot\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\cdot w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\int\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\rho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\right\}\cdot\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\cdot w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}\right\}dxdy|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\rho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\right\}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\right\}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-\sigma_{1}-1}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}++C_{\nu_{3},\delta,\kappa,\gamma,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{3}).

Combining the above estimates, we can obtain that

|J_{6,4}|\leq\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\nu_{3},\kappa,\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{4}),

which, together with the estimate (
3.37
), yields directly

|J_{6}|\leq\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\nu_{3},\kappa,\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{5}).

(3.38)

Substituting the estimates (
3.32
)-(
3.36
)
and (
3.38
) into (
3.31
), we can obtain

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}}\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\nu_{3},\kappa,\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{19}).

Therefore, we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

Finally, let us establish the estimate of tangential derivative of density.
Applying
\partial_{x}^{6}
-operator to the density equation
\eqref{Prandtl-01}_{1}
,
we have

\partial_{t}(\partial_{x}^{6}\varrho^{\varepsilon})+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}(\partial_{x}^{6}\varrho^{\varepsilon})+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}(\partial_{x}^{6}\varrho^{\varepsilon})+\partial_{x}^{6}v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{8}\varrho^{\varepsilon}=Q_{5},

(3.39)

where
Q_{5}
is define as follows

Q_{5}\overset{def}{=}-\sum_{0<k\leq 6}C_{6}^{k}\partial_{x}^{k}u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{7-k}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\sum_{0<k<6}C_{6}^{k}\partial_{x}^{k}v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{6-k}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}.

Recall that
g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}=\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}

and multiplying (
3.28
) by
g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}
, we have

\partial_{t}(\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon})g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}(\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon})g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}(\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon})g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{x}^{6}v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{8}u^{\varepsilon}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{x}^{6}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}=Q_{2}\cdot g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}.

(3.40)

Recall that
\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}=\partial_{x}^{6}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}
,
then it is easy to check that

\partial_{t}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}=Q_{5}-Q_{6}\cdot g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}-Q_{7}\cdot\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}+2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon},

(3.41)

here
Q_{i}(i=6,7)
are defined as follows

\displaystyle Q_{6}\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle Q_{2}+\partial_{x}^{6}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}),

\displaystyle Q_{7}\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{y}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\}}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\left(-u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}-v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+u^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\partial_{xy}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\right)+v^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\right)-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\left\{\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\right\}.

The above equation (
3.41
) is proven in Appendix
C
in detail.
Now, let us establish the estimate for this good quantity
\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}
as follows.

Lemma 3.6
.

Under the condition of (
3.1
),
the solution
(u^{\varepsilon},v^{\varepsilon},w^{\varepsilon})
of regularized system
(
2.1
) will satisfy

\frac{d}{dt}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\nu_{3},\delta,\kappa}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{11}),

where
\nu_{3}
is the small constant that will be chosen later.

Proof.

Multiplying the equation (
3.41
) by
\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}
and
integrating over
\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\frac{1}{2}\int(\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon})^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy+\varepsilon\int|\partial_{x}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy-\underset{J_{7}}{\underbrace{\gamma\int|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}v^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma-1}dxdy}}

(3.42)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{J_{8}}{\underbrace{\int Q_{5}\cdot\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy}}-\underset{J_{9}}{\underbrace{\int(Q_{6}\cdot g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy}}-\underset{J_{10}}{\underbrace{\int(Q_{7}\cdot\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon})\cdot\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy}}.

Deal with
J_{7}
and
J_{8}
terms.
Obviously, it is easy to check that

|J_{7}|\leq C_{\gamma}\|v^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\gamma}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{1}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.43)

and

\displaystyle|J_{8}|\leq

\displaystyle|\sum_{1\leq k\leq 6}C_{6}^{k}\int\partial_{x}^{k-1}\partial_{x}u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{6-k}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\cdot\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy|

(3.44)

\displaystyle+|\sum_{1\leq k\leq 5}C_{6}^{k}\int\partial_{x}^{k-1}\partial_{x}v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{5-k}\partial_{xy}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\cdot\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{x}u^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{0}}\|\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{\gamma}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}+C\|\partial_{x}v^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{-1}}\|\partial_{xy}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{\gamma+1}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{4}).

Deal with
J_{9}
term.

Using Hardy inequality (
A.1
), the Morse-type inequality (
A.5
)
and estimate (
3.3
),
we have

\displaystyle\int(Q_{2}\cdot g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy

(3.45)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}u^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{0}}\|\partial_{x}u^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{\gamma-1}}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}v^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{-1}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{\gamma}}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{1}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{\gamma}}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{H^{2}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{\gamma}}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{H^{2}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{4}).

Using the inequality (
A.6
), one arrives at

\displaystyle\int\left(\partial_{x}^{6}\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\}\cdot g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy

(3.46)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\cdot g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq k\leq 6}C_{6}^{k}\int\partial_{x}^{k-1}\partial_{x}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{x}^{6-k}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\cdot g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2\gamma}dxdy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta,\kappa}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\|_{H^{5}_{0}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{\gamma}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\nu_{3},\delta,\kappa}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta,\kappa}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{H^{2}}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{6}_{0}}^{6})\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{\gamma}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\nu_{3},\kappa,\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{11}).

Therefore, the combination of estimates (
3.45
)
and (
3.46
) yields directly

|J_{9}|\leq\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\nu_{3},\kappa,\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{11}).

(3.47)

Deal with
J_{10}
term
. Obviously, it holds

|J_{10}|\leq\|Q_{7}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}.

(3.48)

Using the lower bound condition (
3.1
), it holds

\displaystyle\|\frac{\partial_{y}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\}\langle y\rangle}{w^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

(3.49)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\frac{(\partial_{y}u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{xy}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon})\langle y\rangle}{w^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}+C_{\delta}(1+\|u^{\varepsilon}-u_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{xy}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}u^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+C_{\delta}\|v^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{H^{2}}+C_{\delta}(1+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{1}})\|\partial_{xy}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{H^{2}_{0}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{1}}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{H^{2}_{0}}+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{1}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+2}\|_{H^{2}_{0}}+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)).

Similarly, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\|\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\left(-u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}-v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right)\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}

(3.50)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(1+\|u^{\varepsilon}-u_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|v^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle+C_{\delta,\kappa}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta,\kappa}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2}),

and

\displaystyle\|u^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\partial_{xy}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\right)\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|v^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\right)\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}^{2}\left\{\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}\right\}\|_{L^{\infty}}

(3.51)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}(1+\|u^{\varepsilon}-u_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}})(\|\partial_{xy}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|v^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{y}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}(1+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{1}})(\|\partial_{xy}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{H^{2}}+\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{H^{2}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{1}}(\|\partial_{y}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+2}\|_{H^{2}}+\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{H^{2}}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2}).

The combination of estimates (
3.49
)-(
3.51
) yields directly

\|Q_{7}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{\delta,\kappa}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2}),

which, together with estimate (
3.48
), yields directly

|J_{10}|\leq C_{\delta,\kappa}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{4}).

(3.52)

Substituting the estimates (
3.43
), (
3.44
),
(
3.47
) and (
3.52
) into (
3.42
), we have

\frac{d}{dt}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\nu_{3},\kappa,\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{11}),

which, together with (
3.4
), yields directly

\frac{d}{dt}\|\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\nu_{3}\|\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\nu_{3},\kappa,\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{11}).

Therefore, we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

3.3.  Weighted
L^{\infty}
-estimates for lower order terms

In order to close the energy estimate, we need to establish the weighted
L^{\infty}
-estimates for lower-order derivatives of
w^{\varepsilon}
and density.
First of all, we will establish the uniform (in
\varepsilon
) weighted
L^{\infty}
-estimates for
\partial^{\alpha}w^{\varepsilon}(|\alpha|\leq 2)
. Through viewing the equation of
\partial^{\alpha}w^{\varepsilon}
as a “linear” parabolic equations, the estimate can be obtained by
using the classical maximum principle stated in Lemmas

A.4
and
A.5
(see Appendix
A
).
For all
|\alpha|\leq 2
, let us define

B_{\alpha}\overset{def}{=}\partial^{\alpha}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+\alpha_{2}},

and

I\overset{def}{=}\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha}|B_{\beta}^{2}|.

Then, we can establish the following estimate.

Lemma 3.7
.

Under the condition of (
3.1
),
the solution
(u^{\varepsilon},v^{\varepsilon},w^{\varepsilon})
of regularized system
(
2.1
) will satisfy

\|\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}|\partial^{\alpha}w^{\varepsilon}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{1}+2\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\left\{\|I(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}+C_{\kappa}t(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{4})\right\}e^{C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2})t}.

Proof.

By a direct computation, the quantity
B_{\alpha}
satisfies

(\partial_{t}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}-\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2})B_{\alpha}=Q_{\alpha}\partial_{y}B_{\alpha}+R_{\alpha}B_{\alpha}+S_{\alpha},

(3.53)

where
Q_{\alpha},R_{\alpha}
and
S_{\alpha}
are defined as follows:

\displaystyle Q_{\alpha}\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})-\frac{2(\sigma_{1}+\alpha_{2})}{\langle y\rangle(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})};

\displaystyle R_{\alpha}\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle(\sigma_{1}+\alpha_{2})\langle y\rangle^{-1}v^{\varepsilon}+\frac{(\sigma_{1}+\alpha_{2})(\sigma_{1}+\alpha_{2}+1)}{\langle y\rangle^{2}(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})}+\frac{(\sigma_{1}+\alpha_{2})\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\langle y\rangle(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}.

If
|\alpha|=0
, then
S_{\alpha}\overset{def}{=}0
. If
|\alpha|>0
, then

\displaystyle S_{\alpha}\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\langle y\rangle^{\beta_{2}-1}\partial_{y}B_{\alpha-\beta+e_{2}}+\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta+e_{2}}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\langle y\rangle^{\beta_{2}-1}B_{\alpha-\beta+e_{2}}

\displaystyle-(\sigma_{1}+\alpha_{2}-\beta_{2}+1)\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\langle y\rangle^{\beta_{2}-2}B_{\alpha-\beta+e_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}u^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\beta_{2}}B_{\alpha-\beta+e_{1}}-\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}v^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\beta_{2}-1}B_{\alpha-\beta+e_{2}}.

Obviously, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle|Q_{\alpha}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{0}}),\quad|R_{\alpha}|\leq C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{1}}+\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{0}}),

(3.54)

\displaystyle|S_{\alpha}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{1}}^{3}+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}})\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}(|B_{\alpha-\beta+e_{2}}|+|B_{\alpha-\beta+e_{1}}|)

\displaystyle+C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{2})\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}|\partial_{y}B_{\alpha-\beta+e_{2}}|.

According to the equation (
3.53
), quantity
I
will satisfy

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}-\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2})I

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}(\varepsilon|\partial_{x}B_{\alpha}|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}|\partial_{y}B_{\alpha}|^{2})+2\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}Q_{\alpha}B_{\alpha}\partial_{y}B_{\alpha}+R_{\alpha}|B_{\alpha}|^{2}+S_{\alpha}B_{\alpha},

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{1}}^{4}+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{4})I,

where we have used the estimate (
3.54
) in the last inequality.
Applying the classical maximum principle inequality (
A.14
)
in Lemma
A.4
for parabolic equation to the quantity
I
under condition
of
\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}
in (
3.1
),
we have

\displaystyle\|I(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq

\displaystyle\max\left\{e^{C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{1}}^{4}+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{4})t}\|I(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})},\right.

(3.55)

\displaystyle\left.\underset{\tau\in[0,t]}{\max}\{e^{C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{1}}^{4}+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{4})(t-\tau)}\|I(\tau)|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}\}\right\}.

Finally, let us estimate the term
\|I(t)|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}
.
Due to the fact that

\|I(t)|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}\leq\|I(0)|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}+t\|\partial_{t}I(t)|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})},

and

\partial_{t}(\partial^{\alpha}w^{\varepsilon})=\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})\right\}-\partial^{\alpha}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}).

For all
|\alpha|\leq 2
, it is easy to check that

(\partial^{\alpha}\{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})\})_{y=0}\leq C_{\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{0}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}^{4})\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+})},

and

(\partial^{\alpha}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\})_{y=0}\leq C(1+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}^{2}).

Thus, we have

\|\partial_{t}I(t)|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}\leq C_{\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{0}}^{8}+\|(w^{\varepsilon},\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})\|_{H^{5}_{1}}^{2}),

and hence, it holds

\|I(t)|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}\leq\|I(0)|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}+C_{\kappa}t(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{0}}^{8}+\|(w^{\varepsilon},\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})\|_{H^{5}_{1}}^{2}).

(3.56)

Thus, substituting the estimate (
3.56
) into (
3.55
),
then we have

\|I(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq\left\{\|I(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}+C_{\kappa}t(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{0}}^{8}+\|(w^{\varepsilon},\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})\|_{H^{5}_{1}}^{4})\right\}e^{C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{5}_{1}}^{4}+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{4})t}.

Therefore, we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

Next, we will establish estimates for the lower bound of vorticity
and the bound of density respectively.

Lemma 3.8
.

Under the condition of (
3.1
),
the solution
(u^{\varepsilon},v^{\varepsilon},w^{\varepsilon})
of regularized system
(
2.1
) will satisfy

\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{\min}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}w(t)\geq\left\{1-T\ \underset{t\in[0,T]}{\max}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t))e^{t\ \underset{t\in[0,T]}{\max}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t))}\right\}\left\{\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{\min}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}w_{0}-C_{\kappa}T(1+\underset{t\in[0,T]}{\max}\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2})\right\},

(3.57)

and

\rho_{0}-CT(1+\underset{t\in[0,T]}{\max}\mathcal{E}_{1}(t))\leq\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}\leq\rho_{0}+CT(1+\underset{t\in[0,T]}{\max}\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)).

(3.58)

Proof.

Let us define
B_{(0,0)}\overset{def}{=}w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}
,
this quantity will satisfy

\partial_{t}B_{(0,0)}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}B_{(0,0)}+\left(v^{\varepsilon}+\frac{2\sigma_{1}\langle y\rangle^{-1}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\right)\partial_{y}B_{(0,0)}-\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}B_{(0,0)}=Q_{8}B_{(0,0)},

where
Q_{8}
is defined as follows

Q_{8}\overset{def}{=}\sigma_{1}\langle y\rangle^{-1}v^{\varepsilon}+\frac{\sigma_{1}(\sigma_{1}+1)\langle y\rangle^{-2}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+\frac{\sigma_{1}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}.

Then, using the classical minimum principle (
A.15
)
in Lemma
A.5
, we obtain

\displaystyle\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{\min}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}w^{\varepsilon}(t)\geq

\displaystyle(1-t\|Q_{8}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}e^{t\|Q_{8}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}})\min\left\{\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{\min}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}w_{0},\underset{[0,T]\times\mathbb{T}}{\min}w^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}\right\}.

(3.59)

Obviously, it is easy to check that

\|Q_{8}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq\underset{[0,T]}{\max}\left\{1+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{1}}+\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{0}}\right\}.

(3.60)

On the other hand, we have

\underset{[0,T]\times\mathbb{T}}{\min}w^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}\geq\underset{\mathbb{T}}{\min}w_{0}|_{y=0}-T\|\partial_{t}w^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{T})}.

(3.61)

Using the vorticity equation
\eqref{Prandtl-02}_{1}
, we can obtain

\|\partial_{t}w^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{T})}=\|\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{T})}.

(3.62)

Due to the fact that

|(\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})_{y=0}|\leq\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\|\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})},

and

|(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon})_{y=0}|\leq C_{\kappa}(\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\|\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}),

it holds

\|\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{T})}\leq C_{\kappa}(1+\underset{[0,T]}{\max}(\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{3}_{1}}^{3}+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{3})).

(3.63)

Substituting the estimates (
3.62
) and (
3.63
)
into (
3.61
), we have

\underset{[0,T]\times\mathbb{T}}{\min}w^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}\geq\underset{\mathbb{T}}{\min}w_{0}|_{y=0}-C_{\kappa}T(1+\underset{[0,T]}{\max}(\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{3}_{1}}^{3}+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{3})),

which, together with estimates (
3.59
) and (
3.60
), yields directly

\displaystyle\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{\min}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}w^{\varepsilon}(t)\geq

\displaystyle\left\{1-T\ \underset{[0,T]}{\max}(1+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{1}}+\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{0}})e^{t\ \underset{[0,T]}{\max}(1+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{1}}+\|\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{0}})}\right\}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{\min}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}w_{0}-C_{\kappa}T(1+\underset{[0,T]}{\max}(\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{3}_{1}}^{3}+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{3}))\right\}.

Finally, we hope to establish the bound for density.
Obviously, it is easy to check that

(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})|_{t=0}-t\|\partial_{t}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}\leq(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})|_{t=0}+t\|\partial_{t}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}.

(3.64)

Due to the density equation
\eqref{Prandtl-01}_{1}
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}

(3.65)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{0}}+C(1+\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{1}})\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{3}_{0}}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\|(\varrho^{\varepsilon},w^{\varepsilon})\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{2}).

The combination of (
3.64
) and (
3.65
) yields directly

\rho_{0}-CT(1+\underset{t\in[0,T]}{\max}\|(\varrho^{\varepsilon},w^{\varepsilon})(t)\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{2})\leq\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}\leq\rho_{0}+CT(1+\underset{t\in[0,T]}{\max}\|(\varrho^{\varepsilon},w^{\varepsilon})(t)\|_{H^{4}_{1}}^{2}).

Therefore, we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

3.4.  Proof of Proposition
3.1

In this subsection, we will give the proof of Proposition
3.1
.
Indeed, due to the the upper bound of
\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}

and smallness of
\nu_{i}(i=1,2,3)
, the combination of estimates obtained in Lemmas
3.3
-
3.7
yields directly

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{D}_{1}(\tau)d\tau\leq\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)+C_{\bar{\kappa},\kappa,\gamma,\delta,\sigma_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{20})d\tau+\left\{\|I(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}+C_{\kappa}t(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{4})\right\}e^{C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2})t}.

Therefore, we complete the proof of Proposition
3.1
.

4
Existence and uniqueness of the original unsteady Prandtl equations

In this section, we will establish the local-in-time existence and uniqueness
stated in the Theorem
1.1
for the original system
(
1.1
).
Indeed, with the help of uniform estimate (
3.2
)
in Proposition
3.1
and estimates (
3.57
)
and (
3.58
) in Lemma
3.8
, we can obtain
the uniform life-span existence time and the uniform estimate.
Then, one can obtain the solution of original Prandtl equation (
1.1
)
from the approximated system (
2.1
) as
\varepsilon
tends to
0^{+}
.

4.1.  Local-in-time existence of the original system

Step 1: Uniform estimate and life-span time.

For the parameters
R_{0}
,
\delta
,
\kappa
and
\bar{\kappa}
, which will be defined later, we define

\displaystyle T_{*}^{\varepsilon}\overset{\text{def}}{=}

\displaystyle\sup\left\{T\in(0,1]|\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)\leq R_{0},w^{\varepsilon}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\geq\delta,\kappa\leq\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}\leq\bar{\kappa},(t,x,y)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}\right\}.

(4.1)

Then, from the Proposition
3.1
, we conclude
for all
T\leq T_{*}^{\varepsilon}
that

\displaystyle E_{1}(T)+\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{D}_{1}(\tau)d\tau

(4.2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)+C_{\bar{\kappa},\kappa,\delta,\gamma,\sigma_{1}}(1+E_{1}(T)^{20})T+(\|I(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}+C_{\kappa}T(1+E_{1}(T)^{4}))e^{C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa}(1+E_{1}(T)^{2})T_{0}}.

Choose
(\kappa,\bar{\kappa},\delta)\overset{def}{=}(2\kappa_{1},\frac{\kappa_{2}}{2},\frac{\delta_{1}}{2})
,
then (
4.2
) yields directly

\displaystyle E_{1}(T)+\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{D}_{1}(\tau)d\tau

(4.3)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)+C_{\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},\delta_{1},\gamma,\sigma_{1}}(1+E_{1}(T)^{20})T+(\|I(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}+C_{\kappa_{1}}T(1+E_{1}(T)^{4}))e^{C_{{\sigma_{1}},\kappa_{1}}(1+E_{1}(T)^{2})T_{0}}.

Let us choose

T_{1}\overset{def}{=}\min\left\{1,\frac{\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)}{2C_{\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},\delta_{1},\gamma,\sigma_{1}}(1+(4\mathcal{E}_{1}(0))^{20})},\frac{\|I(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}}{C_{\kappa_{1}}(1+(4\mathcal{E}_{1}(0))^{4})},\frac{1}{C_{\sigma_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+(4\mathcal{E}_{1}(0))^{2})}\ln(\frac{\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)}{4\|I(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}})\right\}
and
R_{0}\overset{def}{=}4\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)
,
then the inequality (
4.3
) yields directly

E_{1}(T)+\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{D}_{1}(\tau)d\tau\leq 2\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)=\frac{R_{0}}{2},

(4.4)

for all
T\leq\min\left\{T_{1},T_{*}^{\varepsilon}\right\}
.
Choose
T_{2}\overset{def}{=}\min\left\{T_{1},\frac{1}{6(1+4\mathcal{E}_{1}(0))},\frac{\delta_{1}}{2C_{\kappa_{1}}(1+(4\mathcal{E}_{1}(0))^{2})},\frac{\ln 2}{1+4\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)}\right\}
,
according to estimate (
3.57
), we have

\displaystyle\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{\min}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}w(t)

\displaystyle\geq\left\{1-T(1+E_{1}(T))e^{T(1+E_{1}(T))}\right\}\left\{\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{\min}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}w_{0}-C_{\kappa_{1}}T(1+E_{1}(T)^{2})\right\}\geq\delta_{1}=2\delta,

(4.5)

for all
T\leq\min\left\{T_{2},T_{*}^{\varepsilon}\right\}
.
Choose

T_{3}\overset{def}{=}\min\left\{T_{2},\frac{\kappa_{1}}{C(1+4\mathcal{E}_{1}(0))},\frac{\kappa_{2}}{2C(1+4\mathcal{E}_{1}(0))}\right\}
,
then we have

\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}\geq\rho_{0}-CT(1+E_{1}(T))\geq\kappa_{1}=\frac{\kappa}{2},

(4.6)

and

\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}\leq\rho_{0}+CT(1+E_{1}(T))\leq\kappa_{2}=2\bar{\kappa},

(4.7)

for all
T\leq\min\left\{T_{3},T_{*}^{\varepsilon}\right\}
. This yields
T_{3}\leq T_{*}^{\varepsilon}
.
Indeed otherwise, our criterion about the continuation of the solution
would contradict the definition of
T_{*}^{\varepsilon}
in (
4.1
).
Let us define
T_{0}\overset{def}{=}T_{3}
, then we find the
uniform existence time
T_{0}
(independent of
\varepsilon
)
such that the estimates (
4.4
), (
4.5
),(
4.6
)
and (
4.7
) hold.
Therefore, estimate (
4.4
) and lower semi-continuity of norm yield
the estimate (
1.5
) in Theorem
1.1
.

Step 2: Local-in-time existence.

Using the estimates (
4.4
), we have

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{0}}(\|w^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{6}_{loc}}+\|\varrho^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{6}_{loc}}+\|u^{\varepsilon}-u_{\infty}\|_{H^{6}_{loc}})\leq\sqrt{2}\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

One can also find that

\partial_{t}(w^{\varepsilon},\varrho^{\varepsilon})
and
\partial_{t}u^{\varepsilon}
are uniformly (in
\varepsilon
) in
L^{2}(0,T_{0};H^{4}_{loc})
and
L^{2}(0,T_{0};H^{4}_{loc})
respectively.
Then it follows from a strong compactness argument(see Lemma 4 in
[
47
]
) that

(w^{\varepsilon},\varrho^{\varepsilon},u^{\varepsilon}-u_{\infty})
is compact in
C(0,T_{0};H^{s}_{loc})(s<6)
.
In particular, there exists a sequence
\varepsilon_{n}\rightarrow 0^{+}

and
w,\varrho,u-u_{\infty}\in L^{\infty}([0,T_{0}];H^{6}_{loc})\cap C(0,T_{0};H^{s}_{loc})
such that

\left\{\begin{aligned} w^{\varepsilon_{n}}&\rightarrow w&&\text{ in }C([0,T_{0}];H^{s}_{loc});\\
\varrho^{\varepsilon_{n}}&\rightarrow\varrho&&\text{ in }C([0,T_{0}];H^{s}_{loc});\\
u^{\varepsilon_{n}}-u_{\infty}&\rightarrow u-u_{\infty}&&\text{ in }C([0,T_{0}];H^{s}_{loc}).\end{aligned}\right.

(4.8)

Then, we can define
\rho\overset{def}{=}\varrho+\varrho_{\infty}
.
Using the local uniform convergence of
\partial_{x}u^{\varepsilon_{n}}
, we also have the
pointwise convergence of
v^{\varepsilon_{n}}
: as
\varepsilon_{n}\to 0^{+}
,

v^{\varepsilon_{n}}=-\int^{y}_{0}\partial_{x}u^{\varepsilon_{n}}\,dy\rightarrow-\int^{y}_{0}\partial_{x}u\,dy\overset{def}{=}v.

(4.9)

Therefore, combining (
4.8
)-(
4.9
), one
may justify the pointwise convergences of all terms in the regularized Prandtl equations
(
2.1
). Thus, passing to the limit
\varepsilon_{n}\rightarrow 0^{+}
in
(
2.1
),
we know that the limit
(u,v,\rho)
solves the Prandtl equations (
1.1
)
in the classical sense. Furthermore, it is easy to check that

w(t,x,y)\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}}\geq\delta_{1},\quad\quad\kappa_{1}\leq\rho(t,x,y)\leq\kappa_{2},

for all
(t,x,y)\in[0,T_{0}]\times\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}
, which implies the estimate (
1.4
) in Theorem
1.1
.
Therefore, we complete the proof of the local-in-time existence of solution
and estimates (
1.4
) and (
1.5
)
in Theorem
1.1
.

4.2.  Uniqueness of solution of the original system

In this subsection, we will give the proof for the uniqueness of
inhomogeneous unsteady Prandtl equation (
1.1
).
Let
(u_{1},v_{1},\rho_{1})
and
(u_{2},v_{2},\rho_{2})
be two solutions of the original system (
1.1
) satisfying (
1.4
) and (
1.5
) on the existence time
T_{0}
, constructed in the previous subsection, with the same initial data
(u_{0},\rho_{0})
.
Let us denote
(\tilde{u},\tilde{v},\tilde{\rho})\overset{def}{=}(u_{1},v_{1},\rho_{1})-(u_{2},v_{2},\rho_{2})
,
\tilde{w}\overset{def}{=}\partial_{y}u_{1}-\partial_{y}u_{2}=w_{1}-w_{2}
,

a_{2}\overset{def}{=}\frac{\partial_{y}w_{2}}{w_{2}}
,
b_{2}\overset{def}{=}\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{2}}{w_{2}}
. Then one may check that
\tilde{g}\overset{def}{=}\tilde{w}-a_{2}\tilde{u}=w_{2}\partial_{y}\left(\frac{\tilde{u}}{w_{2}}\right)
,
\tilde{f}\overset{def}{=}\tilde{\rho}-b_{2}\tilde{u}
and they satisfy

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}+u_{1}\partial_{x}+v_{1}\partial_{y}-\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}}{\rho_{1}})\tilde{g}=

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{\rho_{1}}\tilde{w}\partial_{y}a_{2}+\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}\partial_{y}w_{2}a_{2}+(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}}-\frac{1}{\rho_{2}})\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}+\partial_{y}w_{1}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}})-\partial_{y}w_{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho_{2}})

(4.10)

\displaystyle-\tilde{u}\left(\tilde{u}\partial_{x}a_{2}+\tilde{v}\partial_{y}a_{2}+\frac{2}{\rho_{1}}a_{2}\partial_{y}a_{2}-\frac{2\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}}{w_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}}-a_{2}\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}+2a_{2}\frac{(\partial_{y}\rho_{2})^{2}}{\rho_{2}^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle-\tilde{u}\left(a_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}(\frac{\partial_{y}^{3}w_{2}}{w_{2}}-a_{2}\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}}{w_{2}})\right),

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}+u_{1}\partial_{x}+v_{1}\partial_{y})\tilde{f}=

\displaystyle-(\frac{\partial_{y}\tilde{w}}{\rho_{1}}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}\partial_{y}w_{2})\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{2}}{w_{2}}-\tilde{u}\left(\tilde{u}\partial_{x}b_{2}+\tilde{v}\partial_{y}b_{2}-\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{2}}{w_{2}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}}{\rho_{2}}\right).

To derive the
L^{2}
estimates on
\tilde{g}
and
\tilde{f}
, let us first define the cutoff function

\chi_{R}(y)\overset{def}{=}\chi(\frac{y}{R})
for any
R\geq 1
, where
\chi\in C^{\infty}_{c}([0,+\infty))
satisfies the following properties:

0\leq\chi\leq 1,\quad\chi|_{[0,1]}\equiv 1,\quad\text{supp}\chi\subset[0,2],\quad-2\leq\chi^{\prime}\leq 0.

Then
\chi_{R}
has
the following pointwise properties: as
R\to+\infty
,

\chi_{R}\to\mathbf{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{+}},\quad|\chi^{\prime}_{R}|\leq\frac{2}{R}\to 0^{+}\quad\text{ and }\quad|\chi^{\prime\prime}_{R}|\leq O(\frac{1}{R^{2}})\to 0^{+}.

For any
t\in(0,T_{0}]
, multiplying equation
\eqref{e:tilde}_{1}
by
2\chi_{R}\tilde{g}
, and then integrating over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}
, we obtain, via
integration by parts,

\displaystyle\int\chi_{R}\tilde{g}^{2}(t)\,dxdy-\int\chi_{R}\tilde{g}^{2}|_{t=0}\,dxdy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\int^{t}_{0}\int\frac{\chi_{R}}{\rho_{1}}|\partial_{y}\tilde{g}|^{2}dxdyd\tau-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{\tilde{g}\partial_{y}\tilde{g}}{\rho_{1}}|_{y=0}dxd\tau+\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}^{2}}\tilde{g}^{2}|_{y=0}dxd\tau-4\int^{t}_{0}\int\chi_{R}\tilde{g}\frac{\partial_{y}a_{2}\tilde{w}}{\rho_{1}}dxdyd\tau

\displaystyle+2\int^{t}_{0}\int\chi_{R}\tilde{g}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}\partial_{y}w_{2}a_{2}+(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}}-\frac{1}{\rho_{2}})\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}+\partial_{y}w_{1}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}})-\partial_{y}w_{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho_{2}})\right)dxdyd\tau

\displaystyle-2\int^{t}_{0}\int\chi_{R}\tilde{g}\tilde{u}\Big(\tilde{u}\partial_{x}a_{2}+\tilde{v}\partial_{y}a_{2}+\frac{2}{\rho_{1}}a_{2}\partial_{y}a_{2}-\frac{2\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}}{w_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}}-a_{2}\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}+2a_{2}\frac{(\partial_{y}\rho_{2})^{2}}{\rho_{2}^{3}}+a_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}(\frac{\partial_{y}^{3}w_{2}}{w_{2}}-a_{2}\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}}{w_{2}})\Big)dxdyd\tau+\int^{t}_{0}\int\chi^{\prime}_{R}v_{1}\tilde{g}^{2}dxdyd\tau+\int^{t}_{0}\int(\partial_{y}(\frac{\chi^{\prime}_{R}}{\rho_{1}})-\chi^{\prime}_{R}\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}^{2}})\tilde{g}^{2}dxdyd\tau

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{0}\int\chi_{R}\partial_{y}\{\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}^{2}}\}\tilde{g}^{2}dxdyd\tau

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle-2\int^{t}_{0}\int\frac{\chi_{R}}{\rho_{1}}|\partial_{y}\tilde{g}|^{2}dxdyd\tau+\sum_{i=1}^{8}R_{i}.

Since
\partial_{y}\tilde{g}|_{y=0}=0
, we can get that
R_{1}=0
and we only need to deal with the boundary integral
R_{2}
.

\displaystyle|R_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\int^{t}_{0}\int^{1}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\left|\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}^{2}}\tilde{g}^{2}\right|dxdyd\tau+\int^{t}_{0}\int^{1}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\left|\partial_{y}\left(\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}^{2}}\tilde{g}^{2}\right)\right|dxdyd\tau\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\int^{t}_{0}\int\frac{\chi_{R}}{\rho_{1}}|\partial_{y}\tilde{g}|^{2}dxdyd\tau+C_{\kappa_{1}}\left(\|\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}^{2}})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\right)\int^{t}_{0}\int\tilde{g}^{2}dxdyd\tau

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\int^{t}_{0}\int\frac{\chi_{R}}{\rho_{1}}|\partial_{y}\tilde{g}|^{2}dxdyd\tau+C_{\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t))\int^{t}_{0}\|\tilde{g}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau,

where we have used the simple trace estimate:

\int_{\mathbb{T}}|f|\;dx\Big|_{y=0}\leq C\left(\int^{1}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{T}}|f|dxdy+\int^{1}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{T}}|\partial_{y}f|dxdy\right).

It’s easy to check that

\displaystyle\|\langle y\rangle\Big(\tilde{u}\partial_{x}a_{2}+\tilde{v}\partial_{y}a_{2}+2a_{2}\partial_{y}a_{2}-\frac{2\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}}{w_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}}-a_{2}\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}+2a_{2}\frac{(\partial_{y}\rho_{2})^{2}}{\rho_{2}^{3}}

\displaystyle+a_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}(\frac{\partial_{y}^{3}w_{2}}{w_{2}}-a_{2}\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}}{w_{2}})\Big)\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}a_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\tilde{v}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}a_{2}\langle y\rangle^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|a_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}a_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}\Big(\|\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\rho_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|a_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{y}^{2}\rho_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}\rho_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\|a_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}\rho_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+(\|\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\rho_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})(\|\partial_{y}^{3}w_{2}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{1}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|a_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2}).

Therefore, it’s easy to check that

|R_{5}|\leq C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2})\int^{t}_{0}\|\tilde{g}\|_{L^{2}}\ \left\|\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\right\|_{L^{2}}d\tau,

and

\displaystyle|R_{3}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t))\int^{t}_{0}\|\tilde{g}\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{w}\|_{L^{2}}d\tau;

\displaystyle|R_{8}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t))\int^{t}_{0}\|\tilde{g}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau.

Next, we deal with the term
R_{4}
and decompose this term as follows:

R_{4}\overset{def}{=}R_{4,1}+R_{4,2},

where

\displaystyle R_{4,1}

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}2\int^{t}_{0}\int\chi_{R}\tilde{g}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}\partial_{y}w_{2}a_{2}\right)dxdyd\tau;

\displaystyle R_{4,2}

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}2\int^{t}_{0}\int\chi_{R}\tilde{g}\left(\partial_{y}w_{1}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}})-\partial_{y}w_{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho_{2}})+(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}}-\frac{1}{\rho_{2}})\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}\right)dxdyd\tau.

It’s easy to check that

|R_{4,1}|\leq C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}\int^{t}_{0}\|\tilde{g}\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}d\tau.

Integrating by parts and the relation
\partial_{y}\tilde{w}=\partial_{y}\tilde{g}+\partial_{y}(a_{2}\tilde{u})=\partial_{y}\tilde{g}+\partial_{y}a_{2}\tilde{u}+a_{2}\tilde{w}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle|R_{4,2}|=

\displaystyle 2\left|\int^{t}_{0}\int\chi_{R}\tilde{g}\left(\partial_{y}\tilde{w}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}})+\partial_{y}w_{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}}-\frac{1}{\rho_{2}})+(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}}-\frac{1}{\rho_{2}})\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}\right)dxdyd\tau\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left|\int^{t}_{0}\int\left(\chi_{R}\tilde{g}\partial_{y}\tilde{w}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}})-\partial_{y}w_{2}(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}}-\frac{1}{\rho_{2}})\chi_{R}\partial_{y}\tilde{g}-\partial_{y}w_{2}(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}}-\frac{1}{\rho_{2}})\chi^{\prime}_{R}\tilde{g}\right)dxdyd\tau\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left|\int^{t}_{0}\int\left(\chi_{R}\tilde{g}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho_{1}})(\partial_{y}\tilde{g}+\partial_{y}a_{2}\tilde{u}+a_{2}\tilde{w})-\partial_{y}w_{2}\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\rho_{2}\rho_{1}}\chi_{R}\partial_{y}\tilde{g}-\partial_{y}w_{2}\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\rho_{2}\rho_{1}}\chi^{\prime}_{R}\tilde{g}\right)dxdyd\tau\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa_{1}}\|\partial_{y}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{g}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\sqrt{\chi_{R}}\frac{\partial_{y}\tilde{g}}{\sqrt{\rho_{1}}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{y}a_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|a_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{w}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle+C_{\kappa_{1}}\|\partial_{y}w_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\sqrt{\chi_{R}}\frac{\partial_{y}\tilde{g}}{\sqrt{\rho_{1}}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\tilde{g}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\int^{t}_{0}\int\frac{\chi_{R}}{\rho_{1}}|\partial_{y}\tilde{g}|^{2}dxdyd\tau+C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t))\int^{t}_{0}\|(\tilde{g},\tilde{\rho},\tilde{w},\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{-1})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau.

Combining the estimate of
R_{4,1}
and
R_{4,2}
, it holds

|R_{4}|\leq\frac{1}{4}\int^{t}_{0}\int\frac{\chi_{R}}{\rho_{1}}|\partial_{y}\tilde{g}|^{2}dxdyd\tau+C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t))\int^{t}_{0}\|(\tilde{g},\tilde{\rho},\tilde{w},\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{-1})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau.

Then combining the estimates from
R_{1}
to
R_{8}
, one has

\displaystyle\int\chi_{R}\tilde{g}^{2}(t)\,dxdy-\int\chi_{R}\tilde{g}^{2}|_{t=0}\,dxdy

(4.11)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{4}\int^{t}_{0}\int\frac{\chi_{R}}{\rho_{1}}|\partial_{y}\tilde{g}|^{2}dxdyd\tau+C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2})\int^{t}_{0}\|(\tilde{g},\tilde{\rho},\tilde{w},\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{-1})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau+R_{6}+R_{7}.

Next, we deal with the estimate of
\tilde{f}
. Multiplying equation
\eqref{e:tilde}_{2}
by
2\chi_{R}\tilde{f}
, and then integrating over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}
, we obtain,

\displaystyle\int\chi_{R}\tilde{f}^{2}(t)\,dxdy-\int\chi_{R}\tilde{f}^{2}|_{t=0}\,dxdy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\int^{t}_{0}\int(\frac{\partial_{y}\tilde{w}}{\rho_{1}}-\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}\partial_{y}w_{2})\chi_{R}\tilde{f}\,dxdyd\tau-2\int^{t}_{0}\int(\tilde{u}\partial_{x}b_{2}+\tilde{v}\partial_{y}b_{2}-\frac{\partial_{y}\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}w_{2}}{w_{2}})\langle y\rangle\cdot\tilde{f}\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{-1}dxdyd\tau

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{0}\int\chi^{\prime}_{R}v_{1}\tilde{f}^{2}\,dxdyd\tau

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle R_{9}+R_{10}+R_{11}.

Similarly, we can get the following estimate

\displaystyle\int\chi_{R}\tilde{f}^{2}(t)\,dxdy-\int\chi_{R}\tilde{f}^{2}|_{t=0}\,dxdy

(4.12)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\int^{t}_{0}\int\frac{\chi_{R}}{\rho_{1}}|\partial_{y}\tilde{g}|^{2}dxdyd\tau+C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t))\int^{t}_{0}\|(\tilde{f},\tilde{\rho},\tilde{w},\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{-1})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau+R_{11}.

We emphasize that
\tilde{w},\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{-1}
can be controlled by
\tilde{g}
, and
\tilde{\rho}
can be controlled by
\tilde{g}
and
\tilde{f}
, that is

\|(\tilde{w},\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{-1})\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{\delta_{1}}\|\tilde{g}\|_{L^{2}},

and

\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{\delta_{1}}\|(\tilde{g},\tilde{f})\|_{L^{2}}.

Combining the estimates of (
4.11
) with (
4.12
),
we can obtain that

\displaystyle\int\chi_{R}(\tilde{g}^{2}+\tilde{f}^{2})(t,x,y)\,dxdy-\int\chi_{R}(\tilde{g}^{2}+\tilde{f}^{2})|_{t=0}\,dxdy+\int^{t}_{0}\int\frac{\chi_{R}}{\rho_{1}}|\partial_{y}\tilde{g}|^{2}dxdyd\tau

(4.13)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2})\int^{t}_{0}\|(\tilde{f},\tilde{g})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau+R_{6}+R_{7}+R_{11}.

Finally both integrands of
R_{6}
,
R_{7}
can be controlled by a multiple of
\tilde{g}^{2}
and
R_{11}
can be controlled by a multiple of
\tilde{f}^{2}
, which belongs to

L^{1}([0,T];\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})
, so applying Lebesgue’s dominated convergence theorem, we
have

\lim_{R\to+\infty}R_{i}=0,\qquad\text{ for }i=6,7,11.

(4.14)

Using monotone convergence theorem and (
4.14
), we can pass to the limit
R\to+\infty
in
(
4.13
) to obtain:

\displaystyle\|(\tilde{f}(t,x,y),\tilde{g}(t,x,y))\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int^{t}_{0}\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\rho_{1}}}\partial_{y}\tilde{g}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau

(4.15)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\tilde{f}(0,x,y),\tilde{g}(0,x,y))\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2})\int^{t}_{0}\|(\tilde{f},\tilde{g})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau.

Applying Gronwall’s lemma to (
4.15
), we obtain

\|(\tilde{f}(t,x,y),\tilde{g}(t,x,y))\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|(\tilde{f}(0,x,y),\tilde{g}(0,x,y))\|_{L^{2}}^{2}e^{C_{\delta_{1},\kappa_{1}}(1+\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)^{2})t},

which implies
\tilde{f}(t)=\tilde{g}(t)\equiv 0

since
\tilde{f}(0)=\tilde{g}(0)=0
. Since
\tilde{g}=w_{2}\partial_{2}\left(\frac{u_{1}-u_{2}}{w_{2}}\right)\equiv 0
,
we have

u_{1}-u_{2}=qw_{2},

(4.16)

for some function
q\overset{def}{=}q(t,x)
. Using the assumption
w_{2}>0

and Dirichlet boundary condition
(u_{1},v_{1})|_{y=0}\equiv 0
,
we know via (
4.16
) that
q\equiv 0
, and hence,
u_{1}(t,x,y)\equiv u_{2}(t,x,y)
.
Since
v_{1},v_{2}
can be uniquely determined by
u_{1}
and
u_{2}
respectively, we also have
v_{1}(t,x,y)\equiv v_{2}(t,x,y)
.
From
\tilde{f}(t,x,y)\equiv 0
and
\tilde{u}(t,x,y)\equiv 0
, we can obtain
\tilde{\rho}(t,x,y)\equiv 0
, that is
\rho_{1}(t,x,y)\equiv\rho_{2}(t,x,y)
. Therefore we complete the uniqueness of the Prandtl system (
1.1
).

5
A priori estimate for the steady Prandtl equations

In this section, we will establish a priori estimate for the inhomogeneous steady Prandtl equations (
1.3
), which
will play an important role in the local-in-
x
existence result
in Section
6
.
Under the assumption of (
5.3
), one can give
the equivalent relation of
u
near the boundary.
Indeed, due to the conditions
u|_{y=0}=0,\partial_{y}u(x,0)\geq\lambda>0,u>0
and
\underset{y\rightarrow+\infty}{\lim}u=u_{\infty}
,
then there exist two small positive constants
\delta_{0}
and
\xi_{0}
, for any
0<\delta_{2}\leq\delta_{0}
, one can obtain that

u(x,y)\geq\frac{\lambda}{2}y,\quad\forall(x,y)\in[0,L]\times[0,\delta_{2}],

(5.1)

and

u(x,y)\geq\xi_{0},\quad\forall(x,y)\in[0,L]\times[\frac{\delta_{2}}{2},+\infty).

(5.2)

It should be pointed out that
\xi_{0}
may depend on
\delta_{2}
.
Now, we state a priori estimate for the system (
1.3
) as follows.

Proposition 5.1
.

Assume
m\geq 3,\sigma_{2}\geq 2
and
the initial data satisfies
\mathcal{X}(0)<+\infty
.
For any positive constants
\lambda
,
\eta
and
L
, suppose

\partial_{y}u(x,0)\geq\lambda,\quad\forall x\in[0,L],

(5.3)

and

u(x,y)>0,\quad\rho(x,y)\geq\eta,\quad\forall(x,y)\in[0,L]\times(0,+\infty),

(5.4)

hold.
Then, the smooth solution
(\rho,u,v)
of system (
1.3
)
will satisfy

\frac{d}{dx}\mathcal{X}(x)+\mathcal{Y}(x)+C\left(|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\right)_{y=0}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x),

(5.5)

where
P(\mathcal{X}(x))
is the polynomial of

\mathcal{X}(x)
satisfying
P(0)=0
.

5.1.  A priori estimate

In this subsection, we will establish some estimates for us to give the proof of Proposition
5.1
.
First of all, we establish estimate for the good unknown quantity
\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})
and tangential velocity
u
, which will be used frequently.

Lemma 5.2
.

Under the condition of (
5.3
) and (
5.4
), for any smooth solution
(\rho,u,v)
of system
(
1.3
), it holds

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\mathcal{X}(x),

\displaystyle|\alpha|\leq m-1;

(5.6)

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\xi_{0},\eta}\mathcal{X}(x)+\delta_{2}C_{\lambda}\mathcal{Y}(x),

\displaystyle|\alpha|=m;

and

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\leq C(1+\mathcal{X}(x));

(5.7)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{3}u\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{6});

\displaystyle\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{u}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{7});

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}u\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)));

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}u\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x);

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))).

Proof.

Step 1: proof of estimate (
5.6
).

Let us define
\chi(y)\in C^{\infty}_{c}([0,+\infty))
which satisfies the following properties:

0\leq\chi\leq 1,\quad\chi|_{[0,\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}]}\equiv 1,\quad\text{supp}\chi\subset[0,\delta_{2}],\quad-2\leq\chi^{\prime}\leq 0.

For all
|\alpha|\leq m
, we decompose
the term
\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

as follows

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}(\chi+1-\chi)^{2}dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}(1-\chi)^{2}dy+\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\chi^{2}dy

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle K_{1,1}+K_{1,2}.

Due to the lower bound of
u
away from the boundary (
5.2
), we have

|K_{1,1}|\leq C_{\xi_{0}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}{u^{2}}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy.

Integrating by part, it holds

\displaystyle|K_{1,2}|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\chi^{2}dy\right|=\left|\left(y|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\chi^{2}\right)^{+\infty}_{0}-\int_{0}^{+\infty}y\partial_{y}\left\{|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\chi^{2}\right\}dy\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}2y\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\chi^{2}+2y|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\chi\chi^{\prime}dy\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\chi\|_{L_{y}^{2}}(\|y\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\chi\|_{L_{y}^{2}}+\|y\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\chi^{\prime}\|_{L_{y}^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\chi\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+C\|y\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\chi\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+C\|y\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\chi^{\prime}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}.

Combining the estimate of
K_{1,1}
and
K_{1,2}
, we can get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\chi^{2}dy+C\int_{0}^{+\infty}y^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\chi^{2}dy

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{+\infty}y^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\chi^{\prime 2}dy+C_{\xi_{0}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}{u^{2}}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy.

Using the lower bound of
u
near the boundary (
5.1
), we have for
|\alpha|\leq m-1
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy\leq C_{\lambda}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\chi^{2}dy+C_{\xi_{0}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\mathcal{X}(x),

and for
|\alpha|=m
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{2}C_{\lambda}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\chi^{2}dy+C_{\xi_{0}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{2}C_{\lambda}\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\xi_{0},\eta}\mathcal{X}(x),

which implies the estimate
\eqref{e:v/u}_{1}
and
\eqref{e:v/u}_{2}
.

Step 2: proof of estimates
\eqref{e:u}_{1}
-
\eqref{e:u}_{3}
.
Due to the condition
\underset{y\rightarrow+\infty}{\lim}\bar{u}=0
,
it holds

\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\leq C(1+\|\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2})\leq C(1+\|(\bar{u},\partial_{y}\bar{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2})\leq C(1+\mathcal{X}(x)),

which implies the estimate
\eqref{e:u}_{1}
.
Using the equation
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\langle y\rangle^{2}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C(1+\|\rho\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2})\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\sqrt{\rho}u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{3}),

which yields that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{3}u\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}\{\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}\rho\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{4}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\rho\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{4}\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\rho\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\partial_{y}\rho\|_{H_{y}^{1}}^{2}\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{4}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{H_{y}^{1}}^{2}+\|\rho\|_{H_{y}^{1}}^{2}\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{4}\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{H_{y}^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\rho\|_{H_{y}^{1}}^{2}\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\langle y\rangle\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{H_{y}^{1}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{6}).

Therefore we establish the estimate
\eqref{e:u}_{2}
.

Finally, we estimate the term
\frac{\partial_{x}u}{u}
.

\displaystyle\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{u}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{u}(\chi+1-\chi)\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\leq\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{u}\chi\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{u}(1-\chi)\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}

(5.8)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0}}(\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{y}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}).

We first deal with
\|\partial_{x}u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}
.

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}=\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u}u)\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}

(5.9)

\displaystyle\leq\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\frac{v}{u}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\langle y\rangle^{2}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\|(\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u}),\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u}))\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{y}}.

Then we deal with
\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{y}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}
.

\displaystyle\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{y}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle=\|\frac{1}{y}\int_{0}^{y}\partial_{y^{\prime}}\partial_{x}udy^{\prime}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\leq\|\partial_{y}^{2}v\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}

(5.10)

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\frac{v}{u}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y}^{1}}\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y}^{1}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\langle y\rangle\|_{L_{y}^{2}}+\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}u\langle y\rangle\|_{L_{y}^{2}}).

Combining the estimates (
5.9
) and (
5.10
) into (
5.8
) and using the estimates
\eqref{e:v/u}_{1}
,
\eqref{e:u}_{1}
,
\eqref{e:u}_{2}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{u}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{7}),

which implies the estimate
\eqref{e:u}_{3}
.

Step 3: proof of estimates
\eqref{e:u}_{4}
-
\eqref{e:u}_{6}
.

First we estimate the term
\|\partial_{y}^{\alpha_{2}}\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2},|\alpha_{2}|\leq m+1
by induction.
If
\alpha_{2}=0
, then we have

\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq\mathcal{X}(x).

Assume for
0\leq k\leq l\leq m
, it holds

\|\partial_{y}^{k}\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))).

(5.11)

Then

\displaystyle\partial_{y}^{l+1}\partial_{y}u

\displaystyle=\partial_{y}^{l}\partial_{y}^{2}u=\partial_{y}^{l}\{-\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\}

(5.12)

\displaystyle=-\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}^{l}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})-\sum_{0<j\leq l}C_{l}^{j}\partial_{y}^{j}(\rho u^{2})\cdot\partial_{y}^{l-j}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}K_{1,3}+K_{1,4}.

Using Hölder inequality and the estimate
\eqref{e:u}_{1}
, it is easy to check that

\|K_{1,3}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\leq C(1+\|\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y}^{1}}^{2})\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\sqrt{\rho}u\partial_{y}^{l}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\leq C(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{3}).

(5.13)

Using the estimate
\eqref{e:v/u}_{1}
, it holds

\displaystyle\|K_{1,4}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}
