\displaystyle\leq\|\sum_{0<j\leq l}C_{l}^{j}\partial_{y}^{j-1}\partial_{y}(\rho u^{2})\cdot\partial_{y}^{l-j}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\leq C\|\partial_{y}(\rho u^{2})\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l-1}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\mathcal{X}(x)\|\partial_{y}(\rho u^{2})\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l-1}}^{2}.

According to the assumption (
5.11
), we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{u}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}\leq C(\|\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}{\bar{u}}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l-1}}^{2})\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)));

\displaystyle\|\bar{u}^{2}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}\leq C\|\bar{u}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{4}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)));

\displaystyle\|\bar{\rho}\bar{u}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}\leq C\|\bar{\rho}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}\|\bar{u}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)));

\displaystyle\|\bar{\rho}\bar{u}^{2}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}\leq C\|\bar{\rho}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}\|\bar{u}^{2}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))),

which yields directly

\displaystyle\|K_{1,4}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\mathcal{X}(x)\|\partial_{y}(\rho u^{2})\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l-1}}^{2}

(5.14)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\mathcal{X}(x)\|\partial_{y}\{(\bar{\rho}+\varrho_{\infty})(\bar{u}+u_{\infty})^{2}\}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\mathcal{X}(x)(\|\bar{\rho}\bar{u}^{2}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}+\|\bar{\rho}\bar{u}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}+\|\bar{\rho}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}+\|\bar{u}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2}+\|\bar{u}^{2}\|_{\bar{H}_{y,0}^{l}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))).

Combining the estimates (
5.13
) and (
5.14
) into (
5.12
),
we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{l+1}\partial_{y}u\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))),

which yields that

\|\partial_{y}^{\alpha_{2}}\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))),\quad|\alpha_{2}|\leq m+1.

(5.15)

Next, we estimate the term
\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2},|\alpha|\leq m
by induction.

When
|\alpha|=0
, using the relation
\partial_{y}v=\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u}u)=u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})+\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}u
and the boundary condition
\frac{v}{u}|_{y=0}=0
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{v}{u}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{2}).

Assume that for
k\leq m-1
,

\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))),\quad|\alpha|\leq k,

which yields that

\|\bar{u}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{2}\leq C(\|\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}^{\alpha_{2}}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}v\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2})\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))),\quad 1\leq|\alpha_{2}|\leq m,

(5.16)

and for
i=1,2
,

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}u\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}=\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

(5.17)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\rho u^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}(\rho u^{2})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\|\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y}^{1}}^{2})\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\sqrt{\rho}u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta-e_{i}}\partial^{e_{i}}(\rho u^{2})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u}))\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{3}+C(1+\|\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{4}+\|\bar{u}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{8})\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m-2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))).

We first deal with the term
\|\partial^{\alpha+e_{2}}\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}
.

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha+e_{2}}\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}=\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(u\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}^{2}u\}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\partial_{y}u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}\{u\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle K_{1,5}+K_{1,6}+K_{1,7}.

Using the estimate
\eqref{e:v/u}_{1}
and (
5.17
), we can obtain that

\displaystyle K_{1,5}=

\displaystyle\|\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}^{2}\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))).

Using the estimate
\eqref{e:v/u}_{1}
and (
5.16
), we can obtain that

\displaystyle K_{1,6}=

\displaystyle\|\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}u\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))).

Using the Hardy inequality, Sobolev embedding, the estimate
\eqref{e:v/u}_{1}
and (
5.16
), we can obtain that for
i=1,2
,

\displaystyle K_{1,7}\leq

\displaystyle\|u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta-e_{i}}\partial^{e_{i}}u\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{e_{i}}u\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-2}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))).

Thus, the combination of estimates of terms from
K_{1,5}
to
K_{1,7}
yields directly

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha+e_{2}}\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))).

(5.18)

Next, we deal with the term
\|\partial^{\alpha+e_{1}}\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}
. Applying the divergence-free condition as well as the estimates
(
5.6
)
and (
5.18
), we have

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha+e_{1}}\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}\{u\cdot\partial_{xy}(\frac{v}{u})\}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\partial_{y}v\cdot\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\partial_{y}^{2}v\cdot\frac{v}{u}\}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\partial_{y}u\cdot\partial_{x}(\frac{v}{u})\}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{xy}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{e_{i}}u\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}v\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{y}^{2}v\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2}\|\frac{v}{u}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}u\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

Therefore we prove that

\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq\left\{\begin{aligned} &C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))),\quad|\alpha|\leq m,\alpha_{1}\leq m-1;\\
&C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x),\quad|\alpha|=m,\alpha_{1}=m.\end{aligned}\right.

(5.19)

With (
5.15
) and (
5.19
) at hand, we are ready to estimate
\eqref{e:u}_{4}
-
\eqref{e:u}_{6}
.

If
\alpha_{1}=0
, applying the estimate (
5.15
), we have

\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}=\|\partial_{y}^{\alpha_{2}}\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))),

and if
\alpha_{1}\geq 1
and
\alpha_{2}\leq m-1
, using the divergence-free condition and the estimate (
5.19
), it holds

\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}=\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha_{1}-1}\partial_{y}^{\alpha_{2}+1}\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))),

which yields the estimate
\eqref{e:u}_{4}
.
Similar to the above estimate,

\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}u\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}=\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}v\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x),

which yields the estimate
\eqref{e:u}_{5}
.
Similar to the estimate (
5.17
), for
|\alpha|\leq m
,

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}u\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle(1+\|\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y}^{1}}^{2})\|u\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\sqrt{\rho}u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+C(1+\|\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{4}+\|\bar{u}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{8})\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x))),

which yields the estimate
\eqref{e:u}_{6}
.
Therefore, we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

Now, we establish the estimate for the derivatives of density.

Lemma 5.3
.

Under the condition of (
5.3
) and (
5.4
), for any smooth solution
(\rho,u,v)
of system
(
1.3
), it holds

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dx}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{2})+\delta_{2}\mathcal{Y}(x).

Proof.

For any
|\alpha|\leq m
,
applying the
\partial^{\alpha}
differential operator to the
equation
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{1}
, multiplying by
\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{+}
, we have

\frac{d}{dt}\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}|^{2}dy+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}\bar{\rho})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}dy}_{K_{2}}=0.

Denote
K_{2}
as follows.

K_{2}=\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}\bar{\rho}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}dy+\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}\bar{\rho}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}dy\overset{def}{=}K_{2,1}+K_{2,2}.

Integrating by part and using the estimate
\eqref{e:v/u}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle|K_{2,1}|

\displaystyle=|\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}|\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}|^{2}dy|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{v}{u}|\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}|^{2}\right)^{+\infty}_{0}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}|^{2}dy\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{2}).

Using the estimate
\eqref{e:v/u}_{2}
and the Hölder inequality, the term
K_{2,2}
can be estimated as follows

|K_{2,2}|\leq C\|\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\leq(C_{\xi_{0},\eta}\mathcal{X}(x)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}C_{\lambda}\mathcal{Y}(x)^{\frac{1}{2}})\mathcal{X}(x).

Combining the estimates of
K_{2,1}
and
K_{2,2}
, we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

Lemma 5.4
.

Under the condition of (
5.3
) and (
5.4
), for any smooth solution
(\rho,u,v)
of system
(
1.3
), it holds

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dx}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m}\|\sqrt{\rho}u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{u}\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+C\left(|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\right)_{y=0}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x),

where
\mu
is the small constant that will be chosen later.

Proof.

Applying the
\partial_{x}
differential operator to the
equation
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{2}
, using the divergence-free condition, we have

\partial_{x}\{\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\}-\partial_{y}^{3}v=0.

(5.20)

For any
|\alpha|\leq m
, applying the
\partial^{\alpha}
differential operator to the
equation (
5.20
) by
\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\partial^{\alpha}\{\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy}_{K_{3,1}}-\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}^{3}\partial^{\alpha}v\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy}_{K_{3,2}}=0.

Deal with the term
K_{3,1}
.

\displaystyle K_{3,1}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}(\rho u^{2})|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho u^{2}\partial_{x}\{|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial_{x}\left\{\partial^{\beta}(\rho u^{2})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\right\}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho u^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}(\rho u^{2})|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial_{x}\left\{\partial^{\beta}(\rho u^{2})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\right\}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho u^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+K_{3,1,1}+K_{3,1,2}.

Using the estimate
\eqref{e:u}_{3}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle|K_{3,1,1}|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\partial_{x}\rho u^{2}+2\rho u\partial_{x}u)|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy\right|

(5.21)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{x}\rho\|_{H_{y}^{1}}\|u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+C\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\sqrt{\rho}u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{5}).

It is easy to check that

\displaystyle K_{3,1,2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\beta}(\rho u^{2})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{xy}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq\beta\leq\alpha-1}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x}(\rho u^{2})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+2\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\beta}\rho\partial^{\alpha-\beta}(u\partial_{x}u)\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha-1}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x}\rho\partial^{\alpha-\beta}(u^{2})+u^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}\rho\right)\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq i\leq 5}K_{3,1,2,i}.

Using the density equation
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{1}
and integrating by part, the last term can be estimated as follows

\displaystyle K_{3,1,2,5}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{2}\left(\frac{v}{u}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}\rho+\sum_{1\leq\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}\rho\right)\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\alpha}\rho\cdot\partial_{y}\left\{u^{2}\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\right\}dy+\left(\partial^{\alpha}\rho\cdot u^{2}\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\right)^{\infty}_{0}

\displaystyle-\sum_{1\leq\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{2}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}\rho\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\alpha}\rho\cdot\partial_{y}\left\{u^{2}\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\right\}dy

\displaystyle-\sum_{1\leq\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{2}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}\rho\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy.

Using the Hardy inequality, Sobolev embedding and the estimates in Lemma
5.2
, for
i=1,2
,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{1\leq\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{2}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}\rho\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy\right|

(5.22)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial^{e_{i}}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}\|\partial_{y}\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}\|u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{H_{y}^{1}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}\|\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}\|u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta_{2}\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{4}).

Similarly, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\alpha}\rho\cdot\partial_{y}\left\{u^{2}\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\right\}dy\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|u\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\Big(\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}^{2}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\frac{v}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}

\displaystyle+\|\frac{v}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}(\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}-1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}})\Big)

\displaystyle+\|\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\sqrt{u}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\frac{v}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{4}),

which, together with (
5.22
), yields

|K_{3,1,2,5}|\leq\delta_{2}\mathcal{Y}(x)+\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{4}).

Deal with the term
K_{3,1,2,1}
. For
i=1,2
,

\displaystyle|K_{3,1,2,1}|

\displaystyle=|\sum_{1\leq\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\beta-e_{i}}\partial^{e_{i}}\left\{(\bar{\rho}+\varrho_{\infty})(\bar{u}+u_{\infty})^{2}\right\}\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{xy}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy|

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial^{e_{i}}\left((\bar{\rho}+\varrho_{\infty})(\bar{u}+u_{\infty})^{2}\right)\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}\|\partial_{xy}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m-1}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\|\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{2}+\|\bar{u}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{4})\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

Similarly, we arrive at

\displaystyle|K_{3,1,2,2}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{x}\{(\bar{\rho}+\varrho_{\infty})(\bar{u}+u_{\infty})^{2}\}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}\|\partial^{e_{i}}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m-1}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\|\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{2}+\|\bar{u}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{4})\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x);

\displaystyle|K_{3,1,2,3}|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\partial^{\beta}(\bar{\rho}+\varrho_{\infty})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\{(\bar{u}+u_{\infty})\partial_{x}\bar{u}\}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\|\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}})(1+\|(\bar{u},\partial_{x}\bar{u})\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{2})\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{H_{y}^{1}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x);

and

\displaystyle|K_{3,1,2,4}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{x}\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}\|\partial^{e_{i}}\{(\bar{u}+u_{\infty})^{2}\}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{H_{y}^{1}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bar{\rho}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}(1+\|\bar{u}\|_{H_{y,0}^{m}}^{2})\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{H_{y}^{1}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+\delta_{2}\mathcal{Y}(x).

Combining the estimates from terms
K_{3,1,2,i}(i=1,\cdots,5)
, we have

|K_{3,1,2}|\leq\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x),\\

which, together with (
5.21
), yields that

K_{3,1}\geq\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho u^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy-\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)-C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))-C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).\\

(5.23)

Deal with the term
K_{3,2}
.

\displaystyle-K_{3,2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}^{3}\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}^{3}\left\{u\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})+\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha-1}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u\right\}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}\left\{u\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})+2\partial_{y}u\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})+\partial_{y}^{2}u\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\right\}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}^{3}\left\{\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha-1}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u\right\}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq i\leq 4}K_{3,2,i}.

Integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle K_{3,2,1}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}u\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial_{y}\{\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\}dy-\left(u\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\right)_{0}^{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}u|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+2\sigma_{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}-1}dy.

The last term can be estimated as follows:

\displaystyle 2\sigma_{2}|\int_{0}^{+\infty}u\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}-1}dy|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\sigma_{2}}(1+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}})\|\sqrt{u}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\mu,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{2})+C_{\gamma,\sigma_{2}}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{\frac{1}{2}})\mathcal{Y}(x),

which yields directly that

\displaystyle K_{3,2,1}\geq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}u|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy-\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)-C_{\mu,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{2})-C_{\gamma,\sigma_{2}}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{\frac{1}{2}})\mathcal{Y}(x).

Integrating by part, we can obtain that

\displaystyle K_{3,2,2}+K_{3,2,3}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(3\partial_{y}^{2}u\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})+\partial_{y}^{3}u\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\right)\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}2\partial_{y}u\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(3\partial_{y}^{2}u\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})+\partial_{y}^{3}u\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\right)\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\cdot\partial_{y}(\partial_{y}u\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}+2\alpha_{2}})dy-\left(|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\right)_{0}^{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(2\partial_{y}^{2}u\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})+\partial_{y}^{3}u\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\right)\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+2\sigma_{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\cdot\partial_{y}u\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}-1}dy+\left(|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\right)_{y=0}.

Using the estimates in Lemma
5.2
and the Hardy inequality, we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(2\partial_{y}^{2}u\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})+\partial_{y}^{3}u\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\right)\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+2\sigma_{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\cdot\partial_{y}u\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}-1}dy\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\sigma_{2}}(\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}})\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}^{3}u\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}-1}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{4})+C_{\gamma,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}\delta_{2}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{3})\mathcal{Y}(x).

Therefore,

K_{3,2,2}+K_{3,2,3}\geq\left(|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\right)_{y=0}-C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{4})-C_{\gamma,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}\delta_{2}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{3})\mathcal{Y}(x).

Now deal with the term
K_{3,2,4}
.

\displaystyle K_{3,2,4}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha-1}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(\partial_{y}^{3}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u+3\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u+3\partial_{y}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u+\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial_{y}^{3}\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u\right)

(5.24)

\displaystyle\times\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq i\leq 4}K_{3,2,4,i}.

Using the estimates in Lemma
5.2
and the Hardy inequality, we have

\displaystyle|K_{3,2,4,2}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H^{m-1}_{y,\sigma_{1}}}\|\partial_{y}\partial^{e_{i}}u\|_{H^{m-1}_{y,0}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

(5.25)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x);

\displaystyle|K_{3,2,4,3}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H^{m}_{y,\sigma_{1}}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{H^{m}_{y,0}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

Then we deal with the term
K_{3,2,4,1}
.
If
|\alpha|\leq m-1
, for
i=1,2
,

|K_{3,2,4,1}|\leq C\|\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H^{m-2}_{y,\sigma_{2}}}\|\partial^{e_{i}}u\|_{H^{m-2}_{y,0}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+\delta_{2}\mathcal{Y}(x).

If
|\alpha|=m
and
|\beta|\leq m-2
, for
|\gamma|=|(\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2})|=2
,

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=m,|\beta|\leq m-2}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}|\int\partial_{y}^{3}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m-2}}\|\partial^{\gamma}u\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

If
|\alpha|=m
and
\beta=\alpha-e_{2}
, integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}\{\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\}\partial_{y}u\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}(\partial_{y}u\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}})dy-\frac{1}{2}\left(|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\right)_{0}^{\infty}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\right)_{y=0}-C(\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}u\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}})\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\right)_{y=0}-C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{5})-C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{4})\mathcal{Y}(x).

If
|\alpha|=m
and
\beta=\alpha-e_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\beta+e_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\partial_{x}u\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}})\|\frac{\partial_{x}u}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\sqrt{u}\partial^{\beta+e_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{9})+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{4})\mathcal{Y}(x).

Thus, combining the above estimates of
K_{3,2,4,1}
, we have

\displaystyle K_{3,2,4,1}\geq C\left(|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\right)_{y=0}-\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)-C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))-C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

(5.26)

Finally, let us deal with the term
K_{3,2,4,4}
.
If
|\beta|=0
, due to the relation
\partial_{y}^{2}u=-\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})
and
\rho=\bar{\rho}+\varrho_{\infty},u=\bar{u}+u_{\infty}
, integrating by part, for
i=1,2
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}\{\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\rho u^{2})\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq\beta<\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{y}(\rho u^{2})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial^{\beta}(\rho u^{2})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\rho u^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{4}K_{3,2,4,4,i}.

It is easy to check that

\displaystyle|\sum_{i=2}^{4}K_{3,2,4,4,i}|\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\|\partial_{y}(\rho u^{2})\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}\|\partial^{e_{i}}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m-1}}+\|\partial^{e_{i}}(\rho u^{2})\|_{H_{y,0}^{m-1}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m-1}}

\displaystyle+(1+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}^{2})\|\rho\sqrt{u}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\Big)\|\frac{v}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

As for the term
K_{3,2,4,4,1}
,

\displaystyle|K_{3,2,4,4,1}|=

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{2}\frac{v}{u}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}\rho\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq\beta\leq\alpha-1}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{y}\rho\partial^{\alpha-\beta}(u^{2})\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial^{\alpha}\{\rho\partial_{y}(u^{2})\}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x),

where the estimate of the first term can refer to the estimate of
K_{3,1,2,5}
. Therefore, when
|\beta|=0
,

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}^{3}\partial^{\alpha}u\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy|\leq\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

If
|\beta|\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial^{\beta}(\frac{v}{u})\partial_{y}^{3}\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{H_{y,\sigma_{2}}^{m}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{H_{y,1}^{m}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

Thus, combining the above estimates of
K_{3,2,4,4}
, we have

\displaystyle|K_{3,2,4,4}|\leq\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)+C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

(5.27)

Combining the estimates of (
5.25
) (
5.26
) and (
5.27
) into (
5.24
), we can obtain

\displaystyle K_{3,2,4}\geq C\left(|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\right)_{y=0}-\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)-C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))-C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x),

which, together with the estimates of
K_{3,2,i}(i=1,2,3)
, yields

\displaystyle-K_{3,2}\geq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}u|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+C\left(|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\right)_{y=0}

(5.28)

\displaystyle-\mu\mathcal{Y}(x)-C_{\mu,\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))-C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

Combining the estimates of (
5.23
) and (
5.28
), we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

Finally, we will establish the estimate for the lower order derivatives of velocity.

Lemma 5.5
.

Under the condition of (
5.3
) and (
5.4
), for any smooth solution
(\rho,u,v)
of system
(
1.3
), it holds

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dx}\|(\bar{u},\partial_{y}\bar{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{2}).

Proof.

Multiplying
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{3}
by
\bar{u}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\bar{u}\cdot\bar{u}dy+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}v\cdot\bar{u}dy}_{K_{4,1}}=0.

Using Hölder inequality and the estimate (
5.6
), we have

\displaystyle|K_{4,1}|

\displaystyle=|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}(u\frac{v}{u})\cdot\bar{u}dy|

\displaystyle=|\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}\bar{u}\cdot\bar{u}dy+\int_{0}^{+\infty}(u_{\infty}+\bar{u})\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\bar{u}dy|

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}),

which yields that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dx}\|\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{2}).

(5.29)

Applying the
\partial_{y}
differential operator to
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{3}
, multiplying by
\partial_{y}\bar{u}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}\bar{u}\cdot\partial_{y}\bar{u}dy+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}^{2}v\cdot\partial_{y}\bar{u}dy}_{K_{4,2}}=0.

Integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle|K_{4,2}|

\displaystyle=|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}^{2}\bar{u}\cdot\partial_{y}\bar{u}+2\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|\partial_{y}\bar{u}|^{2}+\bar{u}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial_{y}\bar{u}dy|

\displaystyle=|\frac{3}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|\partial_{y}\bar{u}|^{2}+\bar{u}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\cdot\partial_{y}\bar{u}dy|

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{y}\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}),

which yields that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dx}\|\partial_{y}\bar{u}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta}(1+\mathcal{X}(x)^{2}).

(5.30)

Combining the estimates of (
5.29
) and (
5.30
), we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

5.2.  Proof of Proposition
5.1

Combining the estimates from Lemma
5.2
to Lemma
5.5
and choosing
\mu
small enough, we have

\frac{d}{dx}\mathcal{X}(x)+\mathcal{Y}(x)+C\left(|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|^{2}\partial_{y}u\right)_{y=0}\leq C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))+C_{\lambda,\xi_{0},\eta,\sigma_{2}}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+P(\mathcal{X}(x)))\mathcal{Y}(x).

Therefore we finish the proof of Proposition
5.1
.

6
Existence and uniqueness of the steady Prandtl equations

In this section, we will establish the local existence and uniqueness of solution stated in Theorem
1.2
for the steady Prandtl system (
1.3
).
Define
(\rho^{0},u^{0})\overset{def}{=}(\rho_{0},u_{0})
,
and for any
\theta>0
, let us consider the following approximated system
for all
k\geq 1
:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{x}\rho^{k}+q^{k-1}\partial_{y}\rho^{k}=0,\\
&\partial_{x}\{\rho^{k}(u^{k-1}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q^{k}\}-\partial_{y}^{3}\{u^{k-1}q^{k}\}=0,\\
&\partial_{x}u^{k}+\partial_{y}(u^{k-1}q^{k})=0,\\
&(q^{k},u^{k})|_{y=0}=(0,0),\\
&\lim_{y\rightarrow+\infty}(\rho^{k},u^{k},\partial_{y}q^{k})=(\varrho_{\infty},u_{\infty},0),\\
&(\rho^{k},u^{k},q^{k})|_{x=0}=(\rho_{0},u_{0},q_{0}).\end{aligned}\right.

(6.1)

Define
q_{0}(y)\overset{def}{=}-\int_{0}^{y}\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}u_{0}}{\rho_{0}u_{0}^{2}}dy^{\prime}
.
For any fixed
\theta>0
, it is easy to apply the Galerkin method
to construct the local solution
(\rho^{k},q^{k},u^{k})

on
[0,L_{\theta,k}]
(cf.
[
14
]
).
This life-span
L_{\theta,k}
may tend to zero
as
\theta\rightarrow 0^{+}
and
k\rightarrow+\infty
.
Thus, we will establish some uniform estimate
and uniform life-span
[0,L_{a}]
for the solution
(\rho^{k},q^{k},u^{k})
.
Therefore, in section
6.1
, for the fixed constant
\theta
, we will
establish some uniform estimates with respect to the parameter
k
for the approximated system (
6.1
). Then the local existence and uniqueness of original steady Prandtl system are to be investigated in sections
6.2
and
6.3
respectively.

6.1.  Uniform estimate of approximated equations

In this subsection, for the fixed constant
\theta
, we will derive the uniform estimate with respect to
k

for the approximated system (
6.1
).
From the equations
\eqref{e:approximate}_{2}
and
\eqref{e:approximate}_{3}
, we have

\partial_{x}\{\rho^{k}(u^{k-1}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q^{k}+\partial_{y}^{2}u^{k}\}=0,

which yields directly

\rho^{k}(u^{k-1}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q^{k}+\partial_{y}^{2}u^{k}=\rho_{0}(2u_{0}\theta+\theta^{2})\partial_{y}q_{0}\overset{def}{=}r_{0}.

For all
k\geq 1
, define the energy norm and dissipation norm for the solution (
\rho^{k},q^{k},u^{k}
) as following:

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}^{k}(x)\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}(\rho^{k}-\varrho_{\infty})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m}\|\sqrt{\rho^{k}}(u^{k-1}+\theta)\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}q^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|(u^{k}-u_{\infty})\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}u^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{Y}^{k}(x)\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m}\|\sqrt{u^{k-1}}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}.

Define

\lambda_{0}\overset{def}{=}\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{4}.

Due to the assumption condition of initial data
u_{0}(y)
, there exist
two small positive constants
\delta_{0}
and
\xi_{0}
, for any
0<\delta_{2}\leq\delta_{0}
, one can obtain that

u_{0}(y)\geq 2\lambda_{0}y,\quad\forall y\in[0,\delta_{2}],

(6.2)

and

u_{0}(y)\geq 2\xi_{0},\quad\forall y\in[\frac{\delta_{2}}{2},+\infty),

(6.3)

where
\xi_{0}
is a positive constant only depending on
\delta_{2}
.
Now, we will state the following uniform estimate.

Proposition 6.1

(Uniform estimate)
.

Let
k\geq 1
be an integer
and
(\rho^{k},q^{k},u^{k})
be sufficiently smooth solution of the approximated system (
6.1
). Suppose
(\rho_{0},u_{0})
satisfy the conditions (
1.6
)-(
1.7
).
For any
L>0
, assume

u^{k-1}(x,y)>0,\quad\partial_{y}u^{k-1}(x,0)\geq\lambda_{0},\quad\forall(x,y)\in[0,L]\times(0,+\infty),

(6.4)

hold for all
k\geq 1
.
Then, there exists a time
L_{a}
independent of
k
and
\theta
such that the following a priori estimate holds true for all
x\in[0,L_{a}]
:

\sup_{0\leq x\leq L_{a}}\mathcal{X}^{k}(x)+\int_{0}^{L_{a}}\mathcal{Y}^{k}(x)dx\leq 2C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}},

(6.5)

and

\displaystyle\partial_{y}u^{k}(x,0)

\displaystyle\geq 2{\lambda_{0}},\quad\rho^{k}(x,y)\geq\kappa_{3};

(6.6)

\displaystyle u^{k}(x,y)

\displaystyle\geq\left\{\begin{aligned} &\lambda_{0}y,&&\quad\forall y\in[0,\delta_{2}];\\
&\xi_{0},&&\quad\forall y\in[\frac{\delta_{2}}{2},+\infty),\end{aligned}\right.

for all
k\geq 1
.

Proof.

Let
K\geq 1
be a fixed large integer, and let us introduce the auxiliary functions
X^{K}(x)
,
Y^{K}(x)
and
Z^{K}(x)
:

\displaystyle X^{K}(x)\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq K}\mathcal{X}^{j}(x);

\displaystyle Y^{K}(x)\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq K}\mathcal{Y}^{j}(x);

\displaystyle Z^{K}(x)\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq K}\partial_{y}u^{j}(x,0).

For the parameter
R
, which will be defined later, we have

L_{\theta,K}^{*}\overset{def}{=}\sup\{L\in(0,1]|X^{K}(x)\leq R,Z^{K}(x)\geq\lambda_{0},x\in(0,L]\}.

(6.7)

For all
1\leq k\leq K
, let us define the characteristic line

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{d}{dx}X^{k-1}(x,\alpha)=q^{k-1}(x,\alpha),\\
&X^{k-1}(x,\alpha)|_{x=0}=\alpha.\end{aligned}\right.

Thus, on the characteristic line the density equation

\eqref{e:approximate}_{1}
has the following form

\frac{d}{dx}\rho^{k}(x,X^{k-1}(x,\alpha))=0,

which, together with condition (
1.7
), yields directly

\rho^{k}(x,X^{k-1}(x,\alpha))=\rho^{k}(x,\alpha)|_{x=0}=\rho_{0}(\alpha)\geq\kappa_{3}.

This implies directly the uniform estimate

\rho^{k}(x,y)\geq\kappa_{3}.

(6.8)

Similar to the analysis of a priori estimate (
5.5
)
of the steady Prandtl equations (
1.3
), we can obtain for all

x\leq L_{\theta,K}^{*}
,

X^{K}(x)+\int_{0}^{x}Y^{K}(\xi)d\xi\leq C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}+C_{6}\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{x}(1+P(X^{K}))Y^{K}d\xi+C_{7}\int_{0}^{x}(1+P(X^{K}))d\xi,

where the constants
C_{6}
and
C_{7}
are only dependent of
\rho_{0},u_{0},\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}
and
\sigma_{2}
.
Similar to the control of the compatibility condition hold at the corner
(0,0)
(see Appendix
D
in detail), we can check that there exists a constant only depending on the initial data
\rho_{0}
,
u_{0}
such that
X^{k}(0)\leq C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}
. Choosing
R\overset{def}{=}4C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}
,

L_{1}\overset{def}{=}\min\{\frac{C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}}{C_{7}(1+P(4C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}})},1\}
and
\delta_{2}\overset{def}{=}\min\{\frac{1}{4C_{6}^{2}(1+P(4C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}))^{2}},\delta_{0}\}
,
then we have for all
x\leq L_{1}

X^{K}(x)+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{x}Y^{K}d\xi\leq 2C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}=\frac{R}{2}.

(6.9)

Using the equation
\eqref{e:approximate}_{3}
and estimate (
6.9
), we have

\displaystyle\partial_{y}u^{k}(x,0)=u_{0}^{\prime}(0)-\int_{0}^{x}(\partial_{y}^{2}\{u^{k-1}q^{k}\})_{y=0}d\xi,

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{x}|(\partial_{y}^{2}\{u^{k-1}q^{k}\})_{y=0}|d\xi

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle x\|\partial_{y}u^{k-1}|_{y=0}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}q^{k}|_{y=0}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cx(\|\partial_{y}u^{k-1}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}u^{k-1}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}u^{k-1}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}})(\|\partial_{y}q^{k}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}q^{k}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}q^{k}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{8}C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}x,

where the constant
C_{8}
and the following mentioned constants
C_{9}
,
C_{10}
are only dependent of
\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}
.
Thus, choose
L_{2}\overset{def}{=}\min\{\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{2C_{8}C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}},L_{1}\}=\min\{\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{2C_{8}C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}},\frac{C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}}{C_{7}(1+P(4C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}))},1\}
,
we have

\displaystyle\partial_{y}u^{k}(x,0)\geq u_{0}^{\prime}(0)-\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{2}\geq\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{2}=2\lambda_{0}.

(6.10)

Due to the equation
\eqref{e:approximate}_{3}
, one can obtain

u^{k}(x,y)=u_{0}(y)-\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{y}\{u^{k-1}q^{k}\}d\xi,

which, together with the boundary conditions
of
q^{k}
and
u^{k-1}
, yields directly

u^{k}(x,y)=u_{0}(y)-\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{y}\{u^{k-1}q^{k}\}d\xi=u_{0}(y)-\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{y}\partial_{\eta}^{2}\{u^{k-1}q^{k}\}d\xi d\eta.

(6.11)

Thus, the combination of (
6.11
) and (
6.2
) yields

\displaystyle u^{k}(x,y)

\displaystyle\geq u_{0}(y)-\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{y}|\partial_{y}^{2}\{u^{k-1}q^{k}\}|dx\geq\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{2}y-xy\|\partial_{y}^{2}\{u^{k-1}q^{k}\}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{2}y-C_{9}C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}xy,

for all
y\in[0,\delta_{2}]
.
Choose
L_{3}\overset{def}{=}\min\{\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{4C_{9}C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}},L_{2}\}=\min\{\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{4C_{9}C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}},\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{2C_{8}C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}},\frac{C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}}{C_{7}(1+P(4C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}))},1\}
,
then we have

u^{k}(x,y)\geq\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{2}y-\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{4}y=\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{4}y={\lambda_{0}}y,\quad\forall(x,y)\in[0,L_{3}]\times[0,\delta_{2}].

(6.12)

Similarly, choose
L_{4}\overset{def}{=}\min\{L_{3},\frac{\xi_{0}}{C_{10}C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}}\}
,
then the combination of (
6.3
) and (
6.11
) yields

u^{k}(x,y)\geq 2\xi_{0}-x\|\partial_{y}^{2}\{u^{k-1}q^{k}\}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}\geq 2\xi_{0}-C_{10}C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}x={\xi_{0}},\quad\forall(x,y)\in[0,L_{4}]\times[\frac{\delta_{2}}{2},+\infty).

(6.13)

Then, the uniform estimates (
6.9
) and (
6.10
)
yield
L_{4}\leq L_{\theta,K}^{*}
.
Indeed otherwise, our criterion about the continuation of the solution
would contradict the definition of
L_{\theta,K}^{*}
in (
6.7
).
Let us define
L_{a}\overset{def}{=}L_{4}
, then we find the uniform
existence time
L_{a}
(independent of
K
and
\theta
) such that the estimates (
6.9
), (
6.10
), (
6.12
)
and (
6.13
) hold, which implies the estimates (
6.5
) and (
6.6
). Thus we finish the proof of Proposition
6.1
.
∎

6.2.  Local existence of the original system

In this subsection, we will show the solution
(\rho^{k},u^{k},q^{k})
to the approximate system (
6.1
) converges to a solution to the original system (
1.3
)
as
k
tends to infinity and
\theta
tends to zero.
To prove this, we define

\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\overset{def}{=}\rho^{k+1}-\rho^{k}
,

\hat{u}^{k+1}\overset{def}{=}u^{k+1}-u^{k}
and
\hat{q}^{k+1}\overset{def}{=}q^{k+1}-q^{k}
.
Then, the quantity
(\hat{\rho}^{k+1},\hat{u}^{k+1},\hat{q}^{k+1})
satisfies the following system:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{x}\hat{\rho}^{k+1}+q^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{\rho}^{k+1}+\hat{q}^{k}\partial_{y}\rho^{k}=0,\\
&\partial_{x}\{\rho^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}{\hat{q}^{k+1}}\}+\partial_{x}\{\hat{\rho}^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}{q^{k}}\}\\
&\quad+\partial_{x}\{\rho^{k}\hat{u}^{k}(u^{k}+u^{k-1}+2\theta)\partial_{y}{q^{k}}\}-\partial_{y}^{3}(u^{k}\hat{q}^{k+1})-\partial_{y}^{3}(\hat{u}^{k}q^{k})=0,\\
&\partial_{x}\hat{u}^{k+1}=-(\partial_{y}u^{k-1}\hat{q}^{k+1}+u^{k-1}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}+\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k}q^{k+1}+\hat{u}^{k-1}\partial_{y}q^{k+1}),\\
&(\hat{q}^{k+1},\hat{u}^{k+1})|_{y=0}=(0,0),\\
&\lim_{y\rightarrow+\infty}(\hat{\rho}^{k+1},\hat{u}^{k+1},\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1})=(0,0,0),\\
&(\hat{\rho}^{k+1},\hat{u}^{k+1},\hat{q}^{k+1})|_{x=0}=(0,0,0),\end{aligned}\right.

(6.14)

and

\partial_{y}^{2}{\hat{u}^{k+1}}=\hat{\rho}^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}{q^{k+1}}+\rho^{k}\hat{u}^{k}(u^{k}+u^{k-1}+2\theta)\partial_{y}{q^{k+1}}+\rho^{k}(u^{k-1}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}{\hat{q}^{k+1}}.

(6.15)

Step 1:
Under the uniform estimate (
6.5
) and the lower bound (
6.6
),
multiplying the equation
\eqref{e:linear}_{2}
by
\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R^{+}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+\underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\{\rho^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy}_{K_{5,1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\{\hat{\rho}^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q^{k}\}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy}_{K_{5,2}}+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\{\rho^{k}\hat{u}^{k}(u^{k}+u^{k-1}+2\theta)\partial_{y}{q^{k}}\}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy}_{K_{5,3}}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}^{3}(u^{k}\hat{q}^{k+1})\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy}_{K_{5,4}}-\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}^{3}(\hat{u}^{k}q^{k})\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy}_{K_{5,5}}=0.

Deal with the term
K_{5,1}
.
It is easy to check that

\displaystyle|K_{5,1}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\rho^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy|+|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\frac{\partial_{x}(u^{k}+\theta)}{u^{k}+\theta}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy|

\displaystyle\leq C\|(u^{k}+\theta)\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}(\|\partial_{x}\rho^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}+\|\rho^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\frac{\partial_{x}(u^{k}+\theta)}{u^{k}+\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}).

Deal with the term
K_{5,2}
.
Integrating by part and using the equation
\eqref{e:linear}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle K_{5,2}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\hat{\rho}^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\partial_{x}\{(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q^{k}\}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\hat{\rho}^{k+1}q^{k}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q^{k}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}+\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\partial_{y}\{q^{k}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q^{k}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\hat{q}^{k}\partial_{y}\rho^{k}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\partial_{x}\{(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q^{k}\}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy.

Due to the estimate of
u^{k}
in (
6.6
), we can obtain

\|\frac{q^{k}}{u^{k}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\leq\|\frac{q^{k}}{u^{k}}\chi\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\frac{q^{k}}{u^{k}}(1-\chi)\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\\
\leq C_{\lambda_{0}}\|\frac{q^{k}}{y}\chi\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+C_{\xi_{0}}\|q^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\\
\leq C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0}}(\|\partial_{y}{q^{k}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|q^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}),\\

which yields

\displaystyle|K_{5,2}|\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}((1+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{3})\|\frac{q^{k}}{u^{k}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\sqrt{u^{k}}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|(u^{k}+\theta)\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}})

\displaystyle+(1+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}})\|(u^{k}+\theta)\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\hat{q}^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\rho^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}q^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|(u^{k}+\theta)\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}(\|\partial_{x}u^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}q^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|u^{k}+\theta\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{xy}q^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mu\|\sqrt{u^{k}}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+C_{\mu,\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}\|((u^{k}+\theta)\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}},\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k},\hat{\rho}^{k+1})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}.

Deal with the term
K_{5,3}
.
Using the equation
\eqref{e:linear}_{3}
and integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle K_{5,3}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\partial_{y}u^{k-1}\hat{q}^{k}+u^{k-1}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}+\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k-1}q^{k}+\hat{u}^{k-1}\partial_{y}q^{k})(\rho^{k}(u^{k}+u^{k-1}+2\theta)\partial_{y}{q^{k}})\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\{\rho^{k}(u^{k}+u^{k-1}+2\theta)\partial_{y}{q^{k}}\}\hat{u}^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\hat{u}^{k-1}\partial_{y}\{q^{k}\rho^{k}(u^{k}+u^{k-1}+2\theta)\partial_{y}{q^{k}}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\hat{u}^{k-1}q^{k}\rho^{k}(u^{k}+u^{k-1}+2\theta)\partial_{y}{q^{k}}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}dy

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\partial_{y}u^{k-1}\hat{q}^{k}+u^{k-1}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}+\hat{u}^{k-1}\partial_{y}q^{k})(\rho^{k}(u^{k}+u^{k-1}+2\theta)\partial_{y}{q^{k}})\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\{\rho^{k}(u^{k}+u^{k-1}+2\theta)\partial_{y}{q^{k}}\}\hat{u}^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy.

Similar to the estimate of
K_{5,2}
, we have

\displaystyle|K_{5,3}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{\rho_{0},u_{0},\xi_{0},\lambda_{0}}\|\hat{u}^{k-1}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}(\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}+\|\sqrt{u^{k}}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}})

\displaystyle+C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}}(\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}(\|\hat{q}^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\hat{u}^{k-1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}})+\|\hat{u}^{k}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mu\|\sqrt{u^{k}}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+C_{\mu,\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}(\|(\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}},\hat{\rho}^{k+1})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|(\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}\langle y\rangle)\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\hat{u}^{k-1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}).

Deal with the term
K_{5,4}
.

Using the equation (
6.15
) and integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle-K_{5,4}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}\{\partial_{y}^{2}u^{k}{\hat{q}^{k+1}}+2\partial_{y}u^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}+u^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\partial_{y}^{3}u^{k}\hat{q}^{k+1}+3\partial_{y}^{2}u^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}+2\partial_{y}u^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1})\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+(\partial_{y}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2})_{y=0}-\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\partial_{y}^{3}u^{k}\hat{q}^{k+1}+3\partial_{y}^{2}u^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1})\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle+2\sigma_{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}-1}+\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy.

Therefore, it holds

\displaystyle-K_{5,4}\geq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+(\partial_{y}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2})_{y=0}+C_{\sigma_{2}}(\|\partial_{y}^{3}u^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\hat{q}^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle+(\|\partial_{y}^{2}u^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}u^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}})\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\sqrt{u^{k}}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+(\partial_{y}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2})_{y=0}-C_{\sigma_{2},\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}.

Deal with the term
K_{5,5}
.
Integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle K_{5,5}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}\{\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{u}^{k}{q^{k}}+2\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{k}+\hat{u}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{k}\}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{u}^{k}(q^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}+2\sigma_{2}q^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}-1}+2\partial_{y}q^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}})dy

\displaystyle+3\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\hat{u}^{k}\partial_{y}^{3}q^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy.

Then we can obtain that

\displaystyle|K_{5,5}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3},\sigma_{2}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{u}^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}(\|\sqrt{u^{k}}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}+\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}})

\displaystyle+C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}\|(\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mu\|\sqrt{u^{k}}\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+C_{\mu,\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3},\sigma_{2}}(\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|(\hat{\rho}^{k},\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\hat{u}^{k-1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}),

where we have used the following estimate according to the equation (
6.15
)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{u}^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}=

\displaystyle\|(\hat{\rho}^{k}(u^{k-1}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}{q^{k}}+\rho^{k-1}\hat{u}^{k-1}(u^{k-1}+u^{k-2}+2\theta)\partial_{y}{q^{k}}+\rho^{k-1}(u^{k-2}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}{\hat{q}^{k}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}(\|\hat{\rho}^{k}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\hat{u}^{k-1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}{\hat{q}^{k}}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}).

Combining the estimate from
K_{5,1}
to
K_{5,5}
and choosing
\mu
small enough, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+(\partial_{y}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2})_{y=0}

(6.16)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3},\sigma_{2}}(\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|(\hat{\rho}^{k},\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\hat{u}^{k-1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}).

Step 2:

Multiplying the equation
\eqref{e:linear}_{1}
by
\hat{\rho}^{k+1}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R^{+}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}|^{2}dy+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}q^{k}\partial_{y}(\frac{|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}|^{2}}{2})dy}_{K_{6,1}}+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\hat{q}^{k}\partial_{y}\rho_{k}\cdot\hat{\rho}_{k+1}dy}_{K_{6,2}}=0.

Integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle|K_{6,1}|

\displaystyle=|\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}q^{k}|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}|^{2}dy|\leq C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}\|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}.

It is easy to obtain

\displaystyle|K_{6,2}|\leq\|\hat{q}^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\rho^{k}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\leq C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}.

Therefore we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}|^{2}dy\leq C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}\|(\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}\langle y\rangle,\hat{\rho}^{k+1})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}.

(6.17)

Multiplying the equation
\eqref{e:linear}_{3}
by
\hat{u}^{k+1}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R^{+}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\hat{u}^{k+1}|^{2}dy=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\partial_{y}u^{k-1}\hat{q}^{k+1}+u^{k-1}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}+\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k}q^{k+1}+\hat{u}^{k-1}\partial_{y}q^{k+1})\cdot\hat{u}^{k+1}dy

(6.18)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}(\|\hat{q}^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\hat{u}^{k}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k}\|_{L^{2}_{y}})\|\hat{u}^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}.

Applying the
\partial_{y}
operator to
\eqref{e:linear}_{3}
,
multiplying the equation by
\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k+1}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R^{+}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k+1}|^{2}dy

(6.19)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{y}(\partial_{y}u^{k-1}\hat{q}^{k+1}+u^{k-1}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}+\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k}q^{k+1}+\hat{u}^{k-1}\partial_{y}q^{k+1})\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k+1}dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\partial_{y}u^{k-1}\hat{q}^{k+1}+u^{k-1}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}+\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k}q^{k+1}+\hat{u}^{k-1}\partial_{y}q^{k+1})\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{u}^{k+1}dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}(\|\hat{q}^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\hat{u}^{k}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k}\|_{L^{2}_{y}})(\|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\hat{u}^{k}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}{\hat{q}^{k+1}}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}(\|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle,\hat{\rho}^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|(\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}).

Finally, we have

\partial_{x}\hat{u}^{k}=-(\partial_{y}u^{k-2}\hat{q}^{k}+u^{k-2}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}+\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k-1}q^{k}+\hat{u}^{k-2}\partial_{y}q^{k}).

Multiplying the equation by
\hat{u}^{k}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R^{+}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\hat{u}^{k}|^{2}dy

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\partial_{y}u^{k-2}\hat{q}^{k}+u^{k-2}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}+\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k-1}q^{k}+\hat{u}^{k-2}\partial_{y}q^{k})\hat{u}^{k}dy

(6.20)

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\partial_{y}u^{k-2}\hat{q}^{k}+u^{k-2}\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}+\hat{u}^{k-2}\partial_{y}q^{k})\hat{u}^{k}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\hat{u}^{k-1}\partial_{y}\{\partial_{y}q^{k}\hat{u}^{k}+q^{k}\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k}\}dy

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}(\|(\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}\langle y\rangle)\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\hat{u}^{k-1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}).

Combining the estimates (
6.17
)-(
6.20
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}|^{2}+|\hat{u}^{k+1}|^{2}+|\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k+1}|^{2}+|\hat{u}^{k}|^{2}dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}(\|(\hat{\rho}^{k+1},\hat{u}^{k+1},\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle)\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|(\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}\langle y\rangle)\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\hat{u}^{k-1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}),

which, together with (
6.16
), yields that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dx}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}+|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}|^{2}+|\hat{u}^{k+1}|^{2}+|\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k+1}|^{2}+|\hat{u}^{k}|^{2}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dy+C(\partial_{y}u^{k}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2})_{y=0}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3},\sigma_{2}}(\|(\hat{\rho}^{k+1},\hat{u}^{k+1},\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|(\hat{\rho}^{k},\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k},\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}})\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\hat{u}^{k-1}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}).

Step 3:

Define

\phi^{k+1}(x)\overset{def}{=}\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\rho^{k+1}(u^{k}+\theta)^{2}|\partial_{y}\hat{q}^{k+1}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}+|\hat{\rho}^{k+1}|^{2}+|\hat{u}^{k+1}|^{2}+|\partial_{y}\hat{u}^{k+1}|^{2}+|\hat{u}^{k}|^{2})dy,

then we have

\frac{d}{dx}\phi^{k+1}(x)\leq C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3},\sigma_{2}}(\phi^{k+1}(x)+\phi^{k}(x)).

Choosing
x^{*}>0
such that
e^{C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3},\sigma_{2}}x}\leq\frac{3}{2}
for all
0\leq x\leq x^{*}
, we can deduce that for all
K\geq 1
,

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sup_{0\leq x\leq x^{*}}\phi^{k+1}(x)<+\infty.

Recall that
\theta>0
and
\rho^{k+1}\geq\kappa_{3}>0
and the uniform estimate of
\mathcal{X}^{k+1}
, we conclude that there exists
\rho_{\theta},u_{\theta},\partial_{y}q_{\theta}\in L^{\infty}(0,L_{a};L^{2}_{y})
such that

\left\{\begin{aligned} \rho^{k+1}&\to\rho_{\theta}~&&\text{in}~L^{\infty}(0,L_{a};L^{2}_{y});\\
u^{k+1}&\to u_{\theta}~&&\text{in}~L^{\infty}(0,L_{a};L^{2}_{y});\\
\partial_{y}q^{k+1}&\to\partial_{y}q_{\theta}~&&\text{in}~L^{\infty}(0,L_{a};L^{2}_{y}).\end{aligned}\right.

It can be checked that
(\rho_{\theta},u_{\theta},q_{\theta})
solves the following equations (
6.21
) for the fixed constant
\theta>0
.

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{x}\rho_{\theta}+q_{\theta}\partial_{y}\rho_{\theta}=0,\\
&\rho_{\theta}(u_{\theta}+\theta)^{2}\partial_{y}q_{\theta}+\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\theta}=r_{0},\\
&\partial_{x}u_{\theta}+\partial_{y}\{u_{\theta}q_{\theta}\}=0,\\
&(\rho_{\theta},u_{\theta})|_{y=0}=(0,0),\\
&\lim_{y\rightarrow+\infty}(\rho_{\theta},u_{\theta},\partial_{y}q_{\theta})=(\varrho_{\infty},u_{\infty},0),\\
&(\rho_{\theta},u_{\theta},q_{\theta})|_{x=0}=(\rho_{0},u_{0},q_{0}).\\
\end{aligned}\right.

(6.21)

Define the energy norm and dissipation norm for the solution (
\rho_{\theta},q_{\theta},u_{\theta}
) as following,

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}_{\theta}(x)\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\bar{\rho}_{\theta}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m}\|\sqrt{\rho_{\theta}}(u_{\theta}+\theta)\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}q_{\theta}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\bar{u}_{\theta}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}\bar{u}_{\theta}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2};

\displaystyle\mathcal{Y}_{\theta}(x)\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m}\|\sqrt{u_{\theta}}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}q_{\theta}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{y}^{2}}^{2}.

Then, by virtue of semi-continuity of norm, we deduce from the uniform bound (
6.5
) and the lower bound (
6.6
) that
(\rho_{\theta},u_{\theta},q_{\theta})
satisfies the estimates

\sup_{0\leq x\leq L_{a}}\mathcal{X}_{\theta}(x)+\int_{0}^{L_{a}}\mathcal{Y}_{\theta}(x)dx\leq 2C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}},

(6.22)

and

u_{\theta}(x,y)>0,\quad\rho_{\theta}(x,y)\geq\kappa_{3},\quad\partial_{y}u_{\theta}(x,0)\geq\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{2},\forall(x,y)\in[0,L_{a}]\times(0,+\infty).

(6.23)

Similar to the analysis of a priori estimate of the steady Prandtl equations (
1.3
) in Section
5
, we can establish a priori estimate and derive the uniform estimate independent of
\theta
for the equation (
6.21
). Here we omit the proof for the sake of simplicity.
Taking
\theta\to 0
, we conclude that there exists solution
(\rho,u,q)

satisfies the following system

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{x}\rho+q\partial_{y}\rho=0,\\
&\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}q+\partial_{y}^{2}u=0,\\
&\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}\{uq\}=0,\\
&(q,u)|_{y=0}=(0,0),\\
&\lim_{y\rightarrow+\infty}(\rho,u,\partial_{y}q)=(\varrho_{\infty},u_{\infty},0),\\
&(\rho,u,q)|_{x=0}=(\rho_{0},u_{0},q_{0}).\\
\end{aligned}\right.

Let us define
v\overset{def}{=}uq
. Therefore
(\rho,u,v)
satisfies the following system:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{x}\rho+\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}\rho=0,\\
&\rho u^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})+\partial_{y}^{2}u=0,\\
&\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0,\\
&(v,u)|_{y=0}=(0,0),\\
&\lim_{y\rightarrow+\infty}(\rho,u)=(\varrho_{\infty},u_{\infty}),\\
&(\rho,u)|_{x=0}=(\rho_{0},u_{0}),\end{aligned}\right.

which is equivalent to the system (
1.3
).
At the same time, due to the uniform estimate (
6.22
), the lower bound (
6.23
) and semi-continuity of norm, we can obtain

\sup_{0\leq x\leq L_{a}}\mathcal{X}(x)+\int_{0}^{L_{a}}\mathcal{Y}(x)dx\leq 2C_{\rho_{0},u_{0}},

and

u(x,y)>0,\quad\rho(x,y)\geq\kappa_{3},\quad\partial_{y}u(x,0)\geq\frac{u_{0}^{\prime}(0)}{2},\quad\forall(x,y)\in[0,L_{a}]\times(0,+\infty),

which yields the estimate (
1.8
) and
(
1.9
) in Theorem
1.2
.
Therefore, we complete the proof of local-in-time existence in Theorem
1.2
.

6.3.  Uniqueness of solution of the original system

In this subsection, we will show the uniqueness of solution to the original system (
1.3
).
Let
(u_{1},v_{1},\rho_{1})
and
(u_{2},v_{2},\rho_{2})
be two solutions in the
existence life-span
[0,L_{a}]
, constructed in the previous subsection, with the same initial data
(u_{0},\rho_{0})
.
Let us denote
(\tilde{u},\tilde{v},\tilde{\rho})\overset{def}{=}(u_{1},v_{1},\rho_{1})-(u_{2},v_{2},\rho_{2})
,

\tilde{q}\overset{def}{=}\frac{\tilde{v}}{u_{2}}
. They satisfy

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\tilde{u}\partial_{x}\rho_{1}+u_{2}\partial_{x}\tilde{\rho}+\tilde{v}\partial_{y}\rho_{1}+v_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{\rho}=0;\\
&\tilde{\rho}(u_{2}\partial_{x}u_{2}+v_{2}\partial_{y}u_{2})+\rho_{1}(\tilde{u}\partial_{x}u_{1}+v_{1}\partial_{y}\tilde{u}-u_{2}^{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q})-\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{u}=0,\end{aligned}\right.

(6.24)

with the zero boundary condition and initial data

(\tilde{u},\tilde{v})|_{y=0}=(0,0),\quad\lim_{y\rightarrow+\infty}(\tilde{\rho},\tilde{u})=(0,0),\quad(\tilde{\rho},\tilde{u})|_{x=0}=(0,0).

Taking
\partial_{x}
-operator to the above equation
\eqref{equ-tilde}_{2}
and
using the divergence-free condition, we have

\partial_{x}\{\rho_{1}u_{2}^{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\}-\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{v}=\partial_{x}\{\tilde{\rho}(u_{2}\partial_{x}u_{2}+v_{2}\partial_{y}u_{2})+\rho_{1}(\tilde{u}\partial_{x}u_{1}+v_{1}\partial_{y}\tilde{u})\}.

(6.25)

Next, we will establish the following estimate, which is useful to prove the uniqueness of the system (
1.3
).

Proposition 6.2
.

Let
(u_{1},v_{1},\rho_{1})
and
(u_{2},v_{2},\rho_{2})
be two solutions of the equation (
1.3
)
with the same initial data
(u_{0},\rho_{0})

satisfying the estimate (
1.8
) and (
1.9
).
Then, the quantity
(\tilde{u},\tilde{v},\tilde{\rho},\tilde{q})
satisfies

\displaystyle\|(\sqrt{\rho_{1}}u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}},\tilde{\rho})(x,y)\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{x}\|(\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}})(\tau,y)\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}d\tau

(6.26)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3},\sigma_{2},L_{a}}\int_{0}^{x}\|(\sqrt{\rho_{1}}u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}},\tilde{\rho})(\tau,y)\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}d\tau,

for any
x\in(0,L_{a}]
.

Proof of Uniqueness.
.

Applying the Gronwall’s lemma to the estimate (
6.26
),
we obtain

(\sqrt{\rho_{1}}u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}},\tilde{\rho})(x,y)\equiv 0.

Then we have
\rho_{1}(x,y)\equiv\rho_{2}(x,y)
.
Due to the fact
\rho_{1}>0
and
u_{2}>0
in the fluid domain, then we have

v_{2}-v_{1}=u_{2}w,

for some function
w\overset{def}{=}w(x)
.
Using the assumption
u_{2}>0
in the fluid domain
and boundary condition
\tilde{v}|_{y=0}=0
,
we know that
w(x,y)\equiv 0
and hence
v_{1}(x,y)\equiv v_{2}(x,y)
.
Using the divergence-free condition, it holds

u_{2}(x,y)-u_{1}(x,y)=\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{\tau}\{u_{2}(\tau,y)-u_{1}(\tau,y)\}d\tau=-\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{y}\{v_{2}(\tau,y)-v_{1}(\tau,y)\}d\tau=0.

Then, we have
u_{2}(x,y)\equiv u_{1}(x,y)
.
This proves the uniqueness of solution in Theorem
1.2
.
∎

In the rest of this subsection, we will give the proof
