of Proposition
6.2
as follows.

Proof of Proposition
6.2
.

Multiplying the equation (
6.25
)
by
\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}
, integrating over
[0,x]\times[0,+\infty)
,
x\in(0,L_{a}]

and integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\!(\rho_{1}u_{2}^{2}|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}})(x,y)dy+\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}\!\!u_{2}|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau+\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!(\partial_{y}u_{2}|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}|^{2})_{y=0}d\tau

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}(\rho_{1}u_{2}^{2})|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau-2\sigma_{2}\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u_{2}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}\cdot\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}-1}dyd\tau

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{2}|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau-2\sigma_{2}\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}u_{2}|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}-1}dyd\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}^{3}u_{2}\tilde{q}\cdot\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\{\tilde{\rho}(u_{2}\partial_{x}u_{2}+v_{2}\partial_{y}u_{2})\}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\{\rho_{1}(\tilde{u}\partial_{x}u_{1}+v_{1}\partial_{y}\tilde{u})\}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{7}II_{i}.

It’s easy to obtain that

\displaystyle|II_{1}|

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\frac{\partial_{x}u_{2}}{u_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\sqrt{\rho_{1}}u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2});

\displaystyle|II_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma_{2}}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}-1}\|_{L^{2}};

\displaystyle|II_{3}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}^{2}u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2};

\displaystyle|II_{4}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\sigma_{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2};

\displaystyle|II_{5}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}^{3}u_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}\|\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}-1}\|_{L_{x}^{2}L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{y}^{3}u_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Then we deal with the term
II_{6}
.

\displaystyle II_{6}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{x}\{\tilde{\rho}u_{2}^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(u_{2}^{2}\partial_{x}\tilde{\rho}\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})+2\tilde{\rho}\partial_{x}u_{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})u_{2}+\tilde{\rho}u_{2}^{2}\partial_{xy}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\right)\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle II_{6,1}+II_{6,2}+II_{6,3}.

Using the equation
\eqref{equ-tilde}_{1}
and integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle II_{6,1}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\tilde{u}\partial_{x}\rho_{1}+\tilde{v}\partial_{y}\rho_{1}+v_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{\rho})\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\tilde{u}\partial_{x}\rho_{1}+\tilde{v}\partial_{y}\rho_{1})\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau-\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\tilde{\rho}\partial_{y}\left\{v_{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}\right\}dyd\tau

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle II_{6,1,1}+II_{6,1,2}+II_{6,1,3}.

We can check that

\displaystyle|II_{6,1,1}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{L_{a}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{v}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}};

\displaystyle|II_{6,1,2}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{v}\langle y\rangle^{2}\|_{L^{2}}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Then we deal with the term
II_{6,1,3}
.

\displaystyle|II_{6,1,3}|\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}(1+\|u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|v_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\|\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|v_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}v_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|v_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Thus we finish the estimate of the term
II_{6,1}
.
Next we deal with the term
II_{6,2}
and
II_{6,3}
.

\displaystyle|II_{6,2}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}};

\displaystyle|II_{6,3}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{xy}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Combining the estimate of
II_{6}
, we have

\displaystyle|II_{6}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{L_{a}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{v}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}}(\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+C\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\|\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}(1+\|u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|v_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|(\partial_{y}u_{2},\partial_{y}v_{2},u_{2},v_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}(\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{xy}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Similar to the estimate of
II_{6,1}
,

\displaystyle|II_{7}|\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}u_{1}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}\|v_{1}\|_{L_{x}^{2}L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{x}\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}-1}\|_{L_{x}^{2}L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}u_{1}\langle y\rangle\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}+\|\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}-1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}u_{1}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{y}\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{x}v_{1}\|_{L_{x}^{2}L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{y}\tilde{u}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|v_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{L_{a}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{v}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\partial_{x}u_{1}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|v_{1}\|_{L_{x}^{2}L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}u_{1}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}}+\|\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}v_{1}\|_{L_{x}^{2}L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\rho_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|v_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Using the equation
\eqref{equ-tilde}_{1}
,
we have

\frac{\tilde{u}}{u_{2}}\partial_{x}\rho_{1}+\partial_{x}\tilde{\rho}+\frac{\tilde{v}}{u_{2}}\partial_{y}\rho_{1}+\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}}\partial_{y}\tilde{\rho}=0.

Multiplying the above equation by
\tilde{\rho}
, integrating over
[0,x]\times[0,+\infty)
and integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\!\tilde{\rho}^{2}(x,y)dy

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})|\tilde{\rho}|^{2}dyd\tau-\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}\!\!\frac{\tilde{u}}{u_{2}}\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\tilde{\rho}dyd\tau-\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}\!\!\frac{\tilde{v}}{u_{2}}\partial_{y}\rho_{1}\tilde{\rho}dyd\tau

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}II_{8}+II_{9}+II_{10}.

It is easy to check that

\displaystyle|II_{8}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v_{2}}{u_{2}})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}^{2};

\displaystyle|II_{10}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{y}\rho_{1}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}\|\tilde{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{y}\rho_{1}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}.

Let us deal with the term
II_{9}
.

\displaystyle|II_{9}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\frac{\tilde{u}}{u_{2}}\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\frac{\tilde{u}}{u_{2}}\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\chi_{\delta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{\tilde{u}}{u_{2}}\partial_{x}\rho_{1}(1-\chi_{\delta_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0}}(\|\frac{\tilde{u}}{y}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L_{x}^{2}L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|{\tilde{u}}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L_{x}^{2}L_{y}^{2}})\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},L_{a}}(\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L_{x}^{2}L_{y}^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}})\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{v}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}.

Recall the definition
\tilde{q}\overset{def}{=}\frac{\tilde{v}}{u_{2}}
,
then it holds true that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{v}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}u_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}}\|\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}+(1+\|u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\|\partial_{y}u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}u_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{y}^{2}})\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+(1+\|u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}},

and similar to the estimate in Lemma (
5.2
), we can obtain for the constant
\xi_{0}
,

\|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{\xi_{0},\kappa_{3}}\|\sqrt{\rho_{1}}u_{2}\partial_{y}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\delta_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}C_{\lambda_{0}}\|\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}.

By virtue of the uniform estimate (
1.9
) in Theorem
1.2
for the two solutions
(u_{1},v_{1},\rho_{1})
and
(u_{2},v_{2},\rho_{2})
, combining the estimates of
II_{1}
to
II_{10}
, using the smallness of
\delta_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\!\tilde{\rho}^{2}(x,y)dy+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\!(\rho_{1}u_{2}^{2}|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}})(x,y)dy+\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}\!\!u_{2}|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau+\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!(\partial_{y}u_{2}|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}|^{2})_{y=0}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3},L_{a}}\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\tilde{\rho}^{2},\rho_{1}u_{2}^{2}|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}|\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}},|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}})dyd\tau+\frac{1}{4}\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}u_{2}|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\lambda_{0},\xi_{0},\kappa_{3},L_{a}}\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}(\tilde{\rho}^{2},\rho_{1}u_{2}^{2}|\partial_{y}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}})dyd\tau+\frac{1}{2}\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}u_{2}|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{q}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2\sigma_{2}}dyd\tau.

Therefore we have finished the proof of Proposition
6.2
.
∎

Data availability

No data was used for the research described in the article.

Appendix A
Some useful Sobolev inequalities and maximum principles

First, we will state two elementary inequalities without proof (i.e., Lemma
A.1
and Lemma
A.2
below), refer to Lemmas
B.1 and B.2 in
[
37
]
.
Let us first state the Hardy type inequalities and the Sobolev type inequality.

Lemma A.1

(Hardy Type Inequalities)
.

Let
f:\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
.

(i)

If
\lambda>-\frac{1}{2}
and
\underset{y\rightarrow+\infty}{\lim}f(x,y)=0
, then

\|f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq\frac{2}{2\lambda+1}\|\partial_{y}f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})};

(A.1)

(ii)
If
\lambda<-\frac{1}{2}
, then

\|f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq\sqrt{-\frac{1}{2\lambda+1}}\|f|_{y=0}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}-\frac{2}{2\lambda+1}\|\partial_{y}f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}.

(A.2)

Lemma A.2

(Sobolev Type Inequality)
.

Let
f:\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
.
Then there exists a universal constant
C>0
such that

\|f\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq C(\|f\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}).

(A.3)

Then we will introduce some inequalities that will be used frequently in Section
3
.

Lemma A.3
.

For proper functions
f,g
, the following inequalities holds

(i)

\underset{y\rightarrow+\infty}{\lim}fg(x,y)=0
, we have

\int_{\mathbb{T}}fg|_{y=0}dx\leq\|\partial_{y}f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{L^{2}}+\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}g\|_{L^{2}}.

(A.4)

(ii)
For
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
and an integer
m\geq 3
,
any
\alpha=(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}),\tilde{\alpha}=(\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\tilde{\alpha}_{2})

with
|\alpha|+|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq m
, it holds

\|\partial^{\alpha}f\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}g\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}+\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|f\|_{H^{m}_{\lambda_{1}}}\|g\|_{H^{m}_{\lambda_{2}}},

(A.5)

with
\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}=\lambda
.

(iii) Assume
g\geq\kappa
and
g
satisfies:

\underset{y\rightarrow+\infty}{\lim}\partial^{e_{i}}g=0(i=1,2),\underset{y\rightarrow+\infty}{\lim}g=g_{\infty},

where
\kappa
and
g_{\infty}
are the constants.
Denote
\bar{g}\overset{def}{=}g-g_{\infty}
,
we have for all
|\alpha|\geq 1
that

\|\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{1}{g})\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{\kappa}(1+\|\bar{g}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{\lambda}}^{|\alpha|}),

(A.6)

and furthermore, if
\underset{y\rightarrow+\infty}{\lim}f=0
,
we have for all
|\alpha|\geq 1

\|\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{f}{g})\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{\kappa}(1+\|\bar{g}\|_{H^{\max\{|\alpha|,2\}}_{{0}}}^{|\alpha|})\|f\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{\lambda}}.

(A.7)

Proof.

The proof of (
A.4
) and (
A.5
) can be found in the Appendix of
[
33
]
. As for the estimate (
A.6
), we will give the proof by induction.
First of all, if
|\alpha|=1
, then we have

0=\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{g}g\right\}=\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{1}{g})g+\frac{1}{g}\partial^{\alpha}g,

which yields directly

\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{1}{g})=-\frac{1}{g^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}g,

Thus, we have

\|\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{1}{g})\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{\kappa}\|\partial^{\alpha}g\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

(A.8)

Thus, the estimate (
A.8
) implies that (
A.6
)
holds for the case
|\alpha|=1
.
Now, let us assume that (
A.6
) holds for
|\alpha|\geq 1
.
Then, for
i,j=1,2
, we have for
|\tilde{\alpha}|=|\alpha|+1

0=\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\left\{\frac{1}{g}g\right\}=\frac{1}{g}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}g+\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}(\frac{1}{g})g+\sum_{0<\tilde{\beta}<\tilde{\alpha}}C_{\tilde{\alpha}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\partial^{\tilde{\beta}-e_{i}}\partial^{e_{i}}(\frac{1}{g})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\beta}-e_{j}}\partial^{e_{j}}g,

which yields directly

\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}(\frac{1}{g})=-\frac{1}{g^{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}g-\frac{1}{g}\sum_{0<\tilde{\beta}<\tilde{\alpha}}C_{\tilde{\alpha}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\partial^{\tilde{\beta}-e_{i}}\partial^{e_{i}}(\frac{1}{g})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\beta}-e_{j}}\partial^{e_{j}}g.

Thus, using the Morse-type inequality (
A.5
), we have

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}(\frac{1}{g})\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

(A.9)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}g\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+C_{\kappa}\|\partial^{e_{i}}(\frac{1}{g})\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{i-1}}\|\partial^{e_{j}}g\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{\lambda+j-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}g\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+C_{\kappa}(1+\|\bar{g}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{0}}^{|\alpha|})\|\partial^{e_{j}}g\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{\lambda+j-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}(1+\|\bar{g}\|_{H^{|\tilde{\alpha}|}_{\lambda}}^{|\tilde{\alpha}|}).

Thus, the estimate (
A.9
) implies that (
A.6
)
holds for the case
|\tilde{\alpha}|=|\alpha|+1
.
Therefore, the induction implies that the estimate (
A.6
)
for all
|\alpha|\geq 1
.

Finally, let us give the proof for the estimate (
A.7
).
If
|\alpha|=1
, it is easy to check that

|\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{f}{g})|\leq C_{\delta}(|\partial^{\alpha}f|+|f||\partial^{\alpha}g|).

Thus, we apply the Sobolev inequality to obtain for all
|\alpha|=1

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{f}{g})\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

(A.10)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}(\|f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\times\|\partial^{\alpha}g\langle y\rangle^{\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}g\langle y\rangle^{\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+C_{\kappa}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}(1+\|\bar{g}\|_{H^{2}_{0}})\|f\|_{H^{1}_{\lambda}}.

If
|\alpha|=2
,
|\beta|=1
, it is easy to check that

|\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{f}{g})|\leq C_{\kappa}\left\{|\partial^{\alpha}f|+|\partial^{\beta}f||\partial^{\alpha-\beta}g|+|f|(|\partial^{\alpha}g|+|\partial^{\beta}g||\partial^{\alpha-\beta}g|)\right\},

which yields directly

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{f}{g})\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

(A.11)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}\|f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial^{\beta}g\langle y\rangle^{\beta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial^{\beta}g\langle y\rangle^{\beta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial^{\beta}g\langle y\rangle^{\beta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\times\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}g\langle y\rangle^{\alpha_{2}-\beta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha-\beta}g\langle y\rangle^{\alpha_{2}-\beta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{\kappa}\|f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\langle y\rangle^{\alpha_{2}}\partial^{\alpha}g\|_{L^{2}}+C_{\kappa}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{\kappa}(\|\partial^{\beta}f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\beta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial^{\beta}f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\beta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial^{\beta}f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\beta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\times\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}g\langle y\rangle^{\alpha_{2}-\beta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha-\beta}g\langle y\rangle^{\alpha_{2}-\beta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}(1+\|\bar{g}\|_{H^{2}_{0}}^{2})\|f\|_{H^{2}_{\lambda}}.

For all
|\alpha|\geq 3
, it is easy to check that for
i=1,2
,

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{f}{g})=\frac{1}{g}\partial^{\alpha}f+\sum_{0<\beta\leq\alpha}C_{\alpha}^{\beta}\partial^{\beta-e_{i}}\partial^{e_{i}}(\frac{1}{g})\partial^{\alpha-\beta}f.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}(\frac{f}{g})\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

(A.12)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+C_{|\alpha|}\|\partial^{e_{i}}(\frac{1}{g})\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{i-1}}\|f\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{\lambda}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\langle y\rangle^{\lambda+\alpha_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+C_{|\alpha|,\kappa}(1+\|\bar{g}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{{0}}}^{|\alpha|})\|f\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{\lambda}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}(1+\|\bar{g}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{{0}}}^{|\alpha|})\|f\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{\lambda}}.

Thus, the combination of estimate (
A.10
),
(
A.11
) and (
A.12
) yields (
A.7
).
Therefore, we complete the proof of this lemma.

∎

Finally we will state two classical maximum principles that are useful in Section
3
, for parabolic equations, which can be found in
[
37
]
.

Lemma A.4

(Maximum Principle for Parabolic Equations)
.

Let
\varepsilon\geq 0
.
Under the condition (
3.1
) that
\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}
has an upper bound, if
H\in C([0,T];C^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}))\cap C^{1}([0,T];C^{0}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}))

is a bounded function that satisfies the differential inequality

\{\partial_{t}+b_{1}\partial_{x}+b_{2}\partial_{y}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}-\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}\}H\leq fH,~\rm{in}~[0,T]\times\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+},

where the coefficients
b_{1},b_{2}
and
f
are continuous and satisfy

\|b_{2}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}<+\infty~{\rm and}~\|f\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq\lambda,

(A.13)

then for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{\sup}H(t)\leq\max\{e^{\lambda t}\|H(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+})},\underset{\tau\in[0,t]}{\max}\{e^{\lambda(t-\tau)}\|H(\tau)|_{y=0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T})}\}\}.

(A.14)

Lemma A.5

(Minimum Principle for Parabolic Equations)
.

Let
\varepsilon\geq 0
.
Under the condition (
3.1
) that
\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}
has an upper bound,
if
H\in C([0,T];C^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}))\cap C^{1}([0,T];C^{0}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}))

is a bounded function with

\kappa(t)\overset{def}{=}\min\{\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{H(0)},\quad\underset{[0,t]\times\mathbb{T}}{\min}H|_{y=0}\}\geq 0,

and satisfies:

\{\partial_{t}+b_{1}\partial_{x}+b_{2}\partial_{y}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}-\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}\}H=fH,

where the coefficients
b_{1},b_{2}
and
f
are continuous and
satisfy (
A.13
), then for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\underset{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}}{\min H(t)}\geq(1-\lambda te^{\lambda t})\kappa(t).

(A.15)

Appendix B
Boundary reduction of vorticity

In this section, we will give the proof for the boundary reduction
representation (
3.21
).

Step 1:
Proof of the boundary reduction representation
\eqref{bl-redu}_{1}
.

Indeed, from the vorticity equation
\eqref{Prandtl-02}_{1}
, we have

\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}w=\partial_{t}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}-2\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon},

which yields on the boundary
y=0
that

\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}=w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+\frac{2\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}.

(B.1)

Thus, (
B.1
) implies the boundary reduction relation
\eqref{bl-redu}_{1}
.

Step 2:
Proof of the boundary reduction representation
\eqref{bl-redu}_{2}
.

Indeed, the vorticity equation
\eqref{Prandtl-02}_{1}
yields directly

\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}=\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}^{3}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{y}^{4}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}-\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}C_{4}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{5-k}w^{\varepsilon},

which, on the boundary
y=0
, yields

\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}=\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}^{3}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}-\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}C_{4}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{5-k}w^{\varepsilon}.

Indeed, on the boundary
y=0
, it holds

\partial_{y}^{3}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})=3w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-2\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon},

(B.2)

The application of (
B.1
), on the boundary
y=0
, yields directly

\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}=\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\}+2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}(\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}).

Due to the boundary condition
u^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}=v^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}=0
, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varrho^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}=-(u\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon})_{y=0}=\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0},

(B.3)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}=-\partial_{y}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\}|_{y=0}=(-w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon})_{y=0}.

and hence, on the boundary
y=0
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon})-2\partial_{t}\{\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\}-\partial_{t}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}

(B.4)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{x}(\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon})}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}-\frac{{\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}+2\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{-w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}-\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle+2\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\left(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\right)-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}.

Thus, on the boundary
y=0
, it holds

\displaystyle\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})\partial_{x}(\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon})-3{\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+2\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\left(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\right)

(B.5)

\displaystyle+{2\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})+2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}-\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}C_{4}^{k}(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})\partial_{y}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{5-k}w^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle+(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})\left(3w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-2\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\}-2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}(\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle N_{1}.

Thus, (
B.5
) implies the boundary reduction relation
\eqref{bl-redu}_{2}
.

Step 3:
Proof of the boundary reduction representation
\eqref{bl-redu}_{3}
.

Using the vorticity equation
\eqref{Prandtl-02}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{7}w^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}^{5}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}

(B.6)

\displaystyle-\partial_{y}^{6}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}-\sum_{1\leq k\leq 5}C_{6}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{7-k}w^{\varepsilon}.

Then, we will give the estimate for
\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}
in the sequence.
Using the vorticity equation
\eqref{Prandtl-02}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{3}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\}-\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\left\{\partial_{y}^{4}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}-\partial_{t}\left\{\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}C_{4}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{5-k}w^{\varepsilon}\right\},

which, together with (
B.3
), yields directly on the
y=0
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{3}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\}-\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}

(B.7)

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\left\{\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}C_{4}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{5-k}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}.

First, we give the boundary reduction for
\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}
.
Indeed, the vorticity equation
\eqref{Prandtl-02}_{1}
yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}\left\{\partial_{t}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}-\sum_{1\leq k\leq 2}C_{2}^{k}\partial_{t}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}^{2}\left\{\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}+2\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}.

Thus, on the boundary
y=0
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}(w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon})-2\partial_{t}^{2}\left\{\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}-\sum_{1\leq k\leq 2}C_{2}^{k}\partial_{t}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}.

(B.8)

It is easy to check that

\partial_{t}^{2}\{w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\}=\partial_{t}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+2\partial_{t}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\partial_{t}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}.

(B.9)

Obviously, on the boundary
y=0
, the vorticity equation

\eqref{Prandtl-02}_{1}
yields

\partial_{t}w^{\varepsilon}=\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}.

(B.10)

Similarly, it is easy to check that

\partial_{t}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}=\partial_{t}\left\{\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}+\partial_{t}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{t}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\}+\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon},

which, on the boundary
y=0
, yields directly

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{t}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}

(B.11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}+\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{4}w^{\varepsilon},

where we have used the condition

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}=(\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon})_{y=0},

(B.12)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}|_{y=0}=(\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{4}w^{\varepsilon})_{y=0}.

Using the relations (
B.10
) and (
B.11
), on the boundary
y=0
,
the relation (
B.9
) yields

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}(w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon})

(B.13)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\left(-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{4}w^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\left\{-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}+\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{4}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle+2(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon})\cdot\partial_{x}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}.

Finally, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}\left\{\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}=\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+2\partial_{t}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}.

(B.14)

Using the density equation
\eqref{Prandtl-01}_{1}
, then we have

\partial_{t}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})=\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\right\},

and hence, on the boundary
y=0
, it holds

\partial_{t}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})=\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}+\frac{w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}},

(B.15)

and

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})

(B.16)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left\{\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}+\frac{w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{6\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{4}}+2\frac{w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}\right)+\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}(-w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon})+2\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{4}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\left(\varepsilon w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{3}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}))+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon})\right).

Using the vorticity equation
\eqref{Prandtl-02}_{1}
,
on the boundary
y=0
, it holds

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}

(B.17)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left\{\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{y}^{3}\left\{\frac{(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})-\frac{\partial_{y}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\right\}

\displaystyle-\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}\left\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\right\}.

Substituting the equalities (
B.12
), (
B.15
)
(
B.16
) and (
B.17
) into (
B.14
),
we have on the boundary
y=0

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}\left\{\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

(B.18)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{3}\left\{\frac{(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})-\frac{\partial_{y}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})-\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}\left\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\right\}

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{6\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{4}}+2\frac{w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}\right)+\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}(-w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon})+2\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{4}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\left(\varepsilon w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{3}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}))+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}+\frac{w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\right)\left(\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right).

Substituting (
B.4
), (
B.13
) and (
B.18
) into (
B.8
),
on the boundary
y=0
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}

(B.19)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{3}\left\{\frac{(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})-\frac{\partial_{y}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\right\}

\displaystyle-2\varepsilon\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})+2\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}\left\{u^{\varepsilon}_{1}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}_{2}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\right\}

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{6\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{4}}+2\frac{w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}\right)-2\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}(-w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon})+2\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{4}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}

\displaystyle-2\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\left(\varepsilon w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{3}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}))+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\}\right)

\displaystyle-2\left(\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}+\frac{w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\right)\left(\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+2(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon})\cdot\partial_{x}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle+\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\left(-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{4}w^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\left\{-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{4}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle-2\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{2(\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon})^{2}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}-\frac{\partial_{x}^{4}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\right)+\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\Big(\frac{\partial_{x}(\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon})}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}-\frac{{\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\left(\frac{-w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}-\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}\right)+2\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}\left(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\right).

Using the equation (
B.2
), we have on the boundary
y=0
that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{3}\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\}

(B.20)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\{3w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-2\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 3\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\left(\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right)+3w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}\left\{\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle-2\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\left\{\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}-2\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}\left\{\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}.

Using the equation (
B.4
), we have on the boundary
y=0
that

\displaystyle\varepsilon\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}

(B.21)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\Bigg\{\partial_{x}(\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon})-\frac{{\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+2\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}(\frac{-w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}-\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}})

\displaystyle+2\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}))-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}\Bigg\}.

Using the density equation
\eqref{Prandtl-01}_{1}

and vorticity equation
\eqref{Prandtl-02}_{1}
, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left\{\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}C_{4}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{5-k}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

(B.22)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}C_{4}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{5-k}\left\{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})-(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}C_{4}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}\left\{\frac{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\right\}\partial_{y}^{5-k}w^{\varepsilon}.

Substituting equations (
B.19
), (
B.20
), (
B.21
),
(
B.22
) into (
B.7
), we have on the boundary
y=0
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}

(B.23)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}\left\{\frac{(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon})\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}+\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}-\frac{\partial_{y}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\right\}

\displaystyle-\frac{2\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}\left\{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\right\}

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{6\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}+2\frac{w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\right)-2\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}(-w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon})+2\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{4}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}

\displaystyle-2\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\left(\varepsilon w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{3}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}))+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon})\right)

\displaystyle-2\left(\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}+\frac{w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\right)\left(\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+2(\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon})\cdot\partial_{x}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle+\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\left(-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{2}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})-(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{4}w^{\varepsilon})\right)

\displaystyle+w^{\varepsilon}(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})\partial_{x}\left\{-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{2}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})-\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{4}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle-2\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{2(\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon})^{2}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}-\frac{\partial_{x}^{4}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\right)+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{\partial_{x}(\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon})}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}-\frac{{\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}\Big)

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{-w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}-\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{3}}\right)+2\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\left(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\right)

\displaystyle+3\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\left(\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right)+3w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}\left\{\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle-2\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\left\{\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}-2\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}^{2}\left\{\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\left\{\partial_{x}(\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon})-\frac{{\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}w^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}+2\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}(\frac{-w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}-\frac{2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}})\right\}

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\left\{2\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}(\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}^{3}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}))-\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{3}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle-\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}C_{4}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{5-k}\left\{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})-(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\partial_{y}^{5}w^{\varepsilon}-\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}C_{4}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}\left\{\frac{u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})^{2}}\right\}\partial_{y}^{5-k}w^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle N_{2}.

Substituting (
B.5
) and (
B.23
) into (
B.6
),
on the boundary
y=0
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}^{7}w^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle(\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty})N_{2}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}N_{1}+\partial_{y}^{5}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})-\sum_{1\leq k\leq 5}C_{6}^{k}\partial_{y}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{y}^{7-k}w^{\varepsilon}.

(B.24)

Thus, (
B.24
) implies the boundary reduction relation
\eqref{bl-redu}_{3}
.
Therefore, we complete the proof of the boundary reduction relation (
3.21
).

Appendix C
Good unknown equations

In this section, we will give the proof for the equations
(
3.30
) and (
3.41
).

Proof of (
3.30
).

Indeed, subtracting (
3.29
) from (
3.27
), we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{t}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{x}^{6}\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\partial_{x}^{6}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})g_{w}^{\varepsilon}

(C.1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q_{1}-Q_{2}\cdot g_{w}^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}(\partial_{t}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}g_{w}^{\varepsilon})+2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}.

It is easy to check that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{x}^{6}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\right\}-\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}(\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}g_{w}^{\varepsilon})\right\}-\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\right\}-\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\right\}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle-\partial_{x}^{6}\partial_{y}\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\}+\partial_{x}^{6}\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}

(C.2)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}-\partial_{y}\left\{\sum_{1\leq k\leq 6}C_{6}^{k}\partial_{x}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{x}^{6-k}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}+\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}(\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}g_{w}^{\varepsilon})\right\}

\displaystyle-\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\right\}-\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\right\}+\sum_{1\leq k\leq 6}C_{6}^{k}\partial_{x}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{x}^{6-k}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}\{\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\}\right\}+Q_{3}^{*},

where
Q_{3}^{*}
is defined as follows

\displaystyle Q_{3}^{*}=

\displaystyle-\partial_{y}\left\{\sum_{1\leq k\leq 6}C_{6}^{k}\partial_{x}^{6}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{x}^{6-k}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}-\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle-\partial_{x}^{6}w^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}\right\}+\sum_{1\leq k\leq 6}C_{6}^{k}\partial_{x}^{k}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}})\partial_{x}^{6-k}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}.

Using the vorticity equation, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}

(C.3)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}}\partial_{t}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}-\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\partial_{t}w^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}})+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}})-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}(\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q_{4}.

Then, substituting the quantities (
C.2
) and (
C.3
)
into (
C.1
), we have

\partial_{t}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{y}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w_{g}^{\varepsilon}\right\}=Q_{1}-Q_{2}\cdot g_{w}^{\varepsilon}-Q_{3}^{*}-Q_{4}\cdot\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}+2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}g_{w}^{\varepsilon}.

Therefore, we complete the proof of equation (
3.30
).
∎

Proof of (
3.41
).

Indeed, subtracting (
3.40
) from (
3.39
), we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho_{g}^{\varepsilon}

(C.4)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q_{5}-Q_{6}\cdot g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{x}^{6}u^{\varepsilon}(\partial_{t}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon})+2\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{7}u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}.

where
Q_{6}
is defined as follows

Q_{6}\overset{def}{=}Q_{2}+\partial_{x}^{6}\left\{\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}\right\}.

Using the density and vorticity equation, we have

\partial_{t}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}g_{\varrho}^{\varepsilon}=\frac{1}{w}\partial_{t}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\partial_{t}w^{\varepsilon}+u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}(\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}})+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}(\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}})-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}(\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}})\overset{def}{=}Q_{7},

(C.5)

where
Q_{7}
is defined as follows

\displaystyle Q_{7}\overset{def}{=}

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{y}(u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}\varrho^{\varepsilon}+v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon})}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\left(-u^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}-v^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\varrho^{\varepsilon}+\varrho_{\infty}}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}w^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+u^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\partial_{xy}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}w^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\right)+v^{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}\partial_{y}w^{\varepsilon}}{(w^{\varepsilon})^{2}}\right)-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{2}(\frac{\partial_{y}\varrho^{\varepsilon}}{w^{\varepsilon}}).

Therefore, substituting the quantity (
C.5
) into (
C.4
),
we complete the proof of (
3.41
).
∎

Appendix D
Compatibility of initial data

In this section, we will give the control of initial data involved vertical
velocity that is not given initial data.
Indeed, we will introduce the compatibility of initial data at the
corner
(0,0)
. This can explain the reason why initial data required
in Theorem
1.2
.
First of all, from the equation
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{2}
, we have

{\rho}u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|_{x=0}=-\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}u}{u}|_{x=0}=-\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}u_{0}}{u_{0}}.

(D.1)

In order to make sure that
\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}u_{0}}{u_{0}}\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})
,
we should at least require

\partial_{y}^{2}u_{0}|_{y=0}=0.

(D.2)

On the other hand, by evaluating the equation
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{2}

at the boundary
y=0
, it is easy to check that

\partial_{y}^{2}u|_{y=0}=0.

Thus, we have
\underset{x\rightarrow 0^{+}}{\lim}\partial_{y}^{2}u|_{y=0}=0
.
Therefore, the initial data condition (
D.2
) essentially
implies the initial compatibility condition at the corner
(0,0)
.

Secondly, from the equation
(
1.3
)
, we have

\rho u\partial_{xy}(\frac{v}{u})=-\partial_{x}\rho u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})-2\rho\partial_{x}u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})+\frac{\partial_{y}^{3}v}{u}=\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}\rho u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})+2\rho\partial_{y}v\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})+\frac{\partial_{y}^{3}v}{u},

which yields directly

\rho u\partial_{xy}(\frac{v}{u})|_{x=0}=\left(\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}\rho u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})+2\rho\partial_{y}v\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\right)_{x=0}+\frac{\partial_{y}^{3}v}{u}|_{x=0}.

From the equation (
1.3
), we have

v=-u\int_{0}^{y}\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}u}{\rho u^{2}}d\tau,

(D.3)

and hence, we define

v_{0}(y)\overset{def}{=}-u_{0}\int_{0}^{y}\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}u_{0}}{\rho_{0}u_{0}^{2}}d\tau.

Thus, we have

\rho u\partial_{xy}(\frac{v}{u})|_{x=0}=\left\{\frac{v}{u}\partial_{y}\rho u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})+2\rho\partial_{y}v\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\right\}|_{x=0}+\frac{\partial_{y}^{3}v_{0}}{u_{0}}.

In order to make sure that

\sqrt{\rho}u\partial_{xy}(\frac{v}{u})|_{x=0}\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})

and avoid the singularity at
y=0
,
we should at least require

\partial_{y}^{3}v_{0}(y)|_{y=0}=0.

(D.4)

On the other hand, from the equation
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{2}

and boundary condition
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{4}
, one may check that

0=\partial_{x}(\rho u\partial_{x}u+\rho v\partial_{y}u-\partial_{y}^{2}u)|_{y=0}=\partial_{y}^{3}v|_{y=0},

which implies

\underset{x\rightarrow 0^{+}}{\lim}\partial_{y}^{3}v|_{y=0}=0
.
Therefore, the initial data condition (
D.4
) essentially
implies the initial compatibility condition at the corner
(0,0)
.
In our paper, the initial compatibility data (
D.4
)
is called the first order generic compatibility condition
at the corner
(0,0)
.
Similarly, taking
\partial_{x}^{m}(m\geq 2)
operator
to the equation
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{2}
, we have

\rho u\partial_{x}^{m}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})=-\frac{1}{u}\sum_{1\leq k\leq m}C^{m}_{k}\partial_{x}^{k}(\rho u^{2})\partial_{x}^{m-k}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})+\frac{\partial_{x}^{m-1}\partial_{y}^{3}v}{u}.

In order to make sure that

\sqrt{\rho}u\partial_{x}^{m}\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})|_{x=0}\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})

and avoid the singularity at
y=0
,
we should at least require

\left(\partial_{x}^{m-1}\partial_{y}^{3}v(x,y)|_{x=0}\right)_{y=0}=0.

(D.5)

On the other hand, we have

0=\partial_{x}^{m}(\rho u\partial_{x}u+\rho v\partial_{y}u-\partial_{y}^{2}u)|_{y=0}=\partial_{x}^{m-1}\partial_{y}^{3}v|_{y=0},

which implies

\underset{x\rightarrow 0^{+}}{\lim}\partial_{x}^{m-1}\partial_{y}^{3}v|_{y=0}=0
.
Therefore, the initial data condition (
D.4
) essentially
implies the initial compatibility condition at the corner
(0,0)
.
In our paper, the initial compatibility data (
D.5
)
is called the m-th order generic compatibility condition
at the corner
(0,0)
.
At the same time, we will explain the initial data
u_{0}
satisfying
this compatibility condition.
From the equation (
D.3
), we have

\displaystyle\partial_{y}^{3}v=

\displaystyle-\partial_{y}^{3}u\int_{0}^{y}\frac{\partial_{y^{\prime}}^{2}u}{\rho u^{2}}dy^{\prime}-\frac{2(\partial_{y}^{2}u)^{2}}{\rho u^{2}}-\partial_{y}u\left(\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho})\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}u}{u^{2}}+\frac{1}{\rho}(\frac{\partial_{y}^{3}u}{u^{2}}-\frac{2\partial_{y}^{2}u\partial_{y}u}{u^{3}})\right)

\displaystyle-\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{1}{\rho})\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}u}{u}-2\partial_{y}(\frac{1}{\rho})\left(\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}u}{u}-\frac{\partial_{y}^{2}u\partial_{y}u}{u^{2}}\right)-\frac{1}{\rho}\left(\frac{\partial_{y}^{4}u}{u}-\frac{2\partial_{y}^{3}u\partial_{y}u}{u^{2}}-\frac{(\partial_{y}^{2}u)^{2}}{u^{2}}+\frac{2\partial_{y}^{2}u(\partial_{y}u)^{2}}{u^{3}}\right).

In order to make sure that
\partial_{y}^{3}v_{0}(y)|_{y=0}=0
,
we require the initial data of
u_{0}
to satisfy

\partial_{y}^{3}u_{0}|_{y=0}=\partial_{y}^{4}u_{0}|_{y=0}=\partial_{y}^{5}u_{0}|_{y=0}=0.

Now, let us consider the initial data

\|\sqrt{\rho}u\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle^{\sigma_{2}}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}}
.
Indeed, from the equation
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{2}
, we have

\rho u\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{v}{u})|_{x=0}=-\left(\partial_{y}\rho u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})+2\rho\partial_{y}u\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\right)_{x=0}-\frac{\partial_{y}^{3}u_{0}}{u_{0}},

(D.6)

and

0=\partial_{y}(\rho u\partial_{x}u+\rho v\partial_{y}u-\partial_{y}^{2}u)|_{y=0}=-\partial_{y}^{3}u|_{y=0}.

(D.7)

In order to avoid the singularity of
y
in (
D.6
), we require the initial data
of
u_{0}
satisfying

\partial_{y}^{3}u_{0}(y)|_{y=0}=0.

(D.8)

The equation (
D.7
) implies directly

\underset{x\rightarrow 0^{+}}{\lim}\partial_{y}^{3}u|_{y=0}=0
.
Therefore, the initial data condition (
D.8
) essentially
implies the initial compatibility condition at the corner
(0,0)
.
Finally, let us control the initial data
\partial_{x}\rho
.
Indeed, from the equation
\eqref{s-Prandtl}_{1}
, it is easy to check that

\|\partial_{x}\rho|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|(\frac{v}{u})|_{x=0}\partial_{y}\rho_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{y}(\frac{v}{u})\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\rho_{0}\|_{L^{2}}

The conditions (
D.1
) and (
D.8
) implies directly

\partial_{x}\rho|_{x=0}\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})
.
Therefore, the initial data
\mathcal{X}(0)

can be controlled of in terms of
\rho_{0}
and
u_{0}
.

Appendix E
Derivative of inhomogeneous Prandtl equations

In this section, we will give a derivation of the Prandtl equations (
1.1
).
We consider the two-dimensional inhomogeneous incompressible Navier-Stokes equations with a small viscosity coefficient
\mu
in a period domain
\{(x,y)|(x,y)\in\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}\}:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}\rho^{\mu}+{\rm div}(\rho^{\mu}\mathbf{u^{\mu}})=0,\\
&\rho^{\mu}\partial_{t}\mathbf{u^{\mu}}+\rho^{\mu}(\mathbf{u^{\mu}}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{u^{\mu}}+\nabla p^{\mu}=\mu\triangle\mathbf{u^{\mu}},\\
&{\rm div}\mathbf{u^{\mu}}=0,\end{aligned}\right.

(E.1)

where
t>0
,
\mathbf{u^{\mu}}=(u^{\mu},v^{\mu})
denotes the velocity, and
p^{\mu}
represents the pressure of fluid. To complete the system (
E.1
), the initial data is given by

\mathbf{u^{\mu}}(0,x,y)=\mathbf{u}^{\mu}_{0}(x,y).

The boundary conditions are given by

u^{\mu}(t,x,0)=v^{\mu}(t,x,0).

(E.2)

As the viscosity coefficient
\mu
tends to zero, we obtain the following system formally.

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}\rho^{0}+{\rm div}(\rho^{0}\mathbf{u^{0}})=0,\\
&\rho^{0}\partial_{t}\mathbf{u^{0}}+\rho^{0}(\mathbf{u^{0}}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{u^{0}}+\nabla p^{0}=0,\\
&{\rm div}\mathbf{u^{0}}=0.\end{aligned}\right.

To find out the terms in (
E.1
) whose contributions are essential for the boundary layer, we use the following scale transformation:

t=t,x=x,\tilde{y}=\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}y,

and set

\displaystyle\rho(t,x,\tilde{y})

\displaystyle=\rho^{\mu}(t,x,y),\quad p(t,x,\tilde{y})=p^{\mu}(t,x,y),

\displaystyle u(t,x,\tilde{y})

\displaystyle=u^{\mu}(t,x,y),\quad v(t,x,\tilde{y})=\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}v^{\mu}(t,x,y).

Taking the leading order, the system (
E.1
) is reduced to

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\rho_{t}+u\partial_{x}\rho+v\partial_{\tilde{y}}\rho=0,\\
&\rho\partial_{t}u+\rho u\partial_{x}u+\rho v\partial_{\tilde{y}}u+\partial_{x}p=\partial_{\tilde{y}}^{2}u,\\
&\partial_{\tilde{y}}p=0,\\
&\partial_{x}u+\partial_{\tilde{y}}v=0.\end{aligned}\right.

(E.3)

The third equation in the system (
E.3
) implies that the leading order of boundary layers for the total pressure
p(t,x,\tilde{y})
is invariant across the boundary layer, and should be matched to the outflow pressure
p(t,x)
on top of boundary layer, that is, the trace of pressure of ideal MHD flow. Hence, we obtain

p(t,x,\tilde{y})\equiv p(t,x).

Furthermore, the density
\rho(t,x,\tilde{y})
, tangential component
u(t,x,\tilde{y})
of velocity flied, should match the outflow density
\rho_{e}(t,x)
and tangential velocity
u_{e}(t,x)
, on the top of boundary layer, that is

\rho(t,x,\tilde{y})\to\rho_{e}(t,x),u(t,x,\tilde{y})\to u_{e}(t,x),\text{as}~\tilde{y}\to+\infty.

Then, we have the following matching conditions:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}\rho_{e}+u_{e}\partial_{x}\rho_{e}=0,\\
&\rho_{e}\partial_{t}u_{e}+\rho_{e}u_{e}\partial_{x}u_{e}+\partial_{x}p=0.\\
\end{aligned}\right.

Moreover, by virtue of the boundary condition (
E.2
), one attains the following boundary condition

u(t,x,0)=v(t,x,0)=0.

Therefore, one can obtain the following Prandtl system

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}\rho+u\partial_{x}\rho+v\partial_{y}\rho=0,\\
&\rho\partial_{t}u+\rho u\partial_{x}u+\rho v\partial_{y}u+\partial_{x}p-\partial_{y}^{2}u=0,\\
&\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0,\\
&(u,v)|_{y=0}=0,\underset{y\rightarrow+\infty}{\lim}(\rho,u)(t,x,y)=(\rho_{e}(t,x),u_{e}(t,x)),\\
&(\rho,u)|_{t=0}=(\rho_{0},u_{0})(x,y),\end{aligned}\right.

here we have replaced
\tilde{y}
by
y
for simplicity of notations.
Thus, we obtain the Prandtl system (
1.1
).
